# Tepna — Documentation Index & Site Map

**The single entry path over the suite's ~60 markdown documents.** The repo accreted a lot of
briefs, audits, and READMEs; this page is the map. Start at the top group and only go as deep as
your task needs.

> **Role legend** — *Authoritative* = a rule you must follow · *Spec* = a format/contract ·
> *Brief* = a build/work plan for one task · *Audit* = a findings report · *Reference* = a guide to
> read · *Narrative* = context, not spec · *Historical* = superseded/done, kept for the record.
> When a *Brief*/*Audit* and an *Authoritative* doc disagree, the authoritative doc wins.

---

## ⚡ At a glance — live surfaces (open these first)

**Here for the product, not the source tree?** These are the live HTML pages a reader actually wants
first — this index maps the ~60 *markdown* docs; the block below surfaces the *HTML*. Open any directly.

- **Front door:** [`index.html`](index.html) — the landing / device picker.
- **Per-node technical references** (what each Dex measures · every metric evidence-graded):
  [OxyDex](OxyDex%20Reference.html) · [PpgDex](PpgDex%20Reference.html) · [PulseDex](PulseDex%20Reference.html) · [ECGDex](ECGDex%20Reference.html) · [HRVDex](HRVDex%20Reference.html) · [GlucoDex](GlucoDex%20Reference.html) · [CPAPDex](CPAPDex%20Reference.html)
- **Architecture & science:** [`Architecture.html`](Architecture.html) · [`Science.html`](Science.html) · [How It's Wired — the Dex Suite](wiring/How%20It's%20Wired%20-%20the%20Dex%20Suite.html)
- **Papers:** [`papers/papers.html`](papers/papers.html) — working preprints, each regenerated from its tool.

> Guide filenames contain spaces (`OxyDex Reference.html`) → every target above is `%20`-encoded so
> crawlers resolve it. Those spaced names are **frozen cross-references** (the badge gate + deploy
> manifest fetch them verbatim) and are deliberately **not** renamed — see
 > [`REPO-DISCOVERABILITY-2026-07-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/REPO-DISCOVERABILITY-2026-07-03-BRIEF.md) §2. *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-04)*

## 0 · Read first (orientation)

> **Repo layout (2026-07-03).** Root holds only base/entry docs + standard OSS files + all runtime/build files (`*.js` / `*.html` / `*.src.html` / `*.css` / `*.json` — never move these). Archival docs live in **`briefs/`** (work-plans + pre-standard kickoffs/handoffs), **`audits/`** (findings, external reviews, fusion issues, validation status, audit prompts), and **`docs/`** (specs, derivations, analysis READMEs, `LEXICON.md`/`EVENT-LEXICON.md`, patterns, deploy + privacy statements, narrative). `ORIENTATION.md` stays in root (the test suite fetches it for the roster gate). New archival docs go straight into the right folder + get a row here.

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CLAUDE.md`](CLAUDE.md) | Project conventions / constitution — the gates, the Clock Contract, the build-then-bundle rule, frozen names | **Authoritative** |
| [`ORIENTATION.md`](ORIENTATION.md) | **Start here** — the 60-second map: the Dex roster, the shared spine, the two gates, and where each fact actually lives (pointer-based, no variable catalog). Roster is gate-backed by a `tests/dex-tests.js` group | Reference *(living)* |
| [`AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Deep-audit charter for an AI/human auditor — this suite's specific bug-classes (units, clock, fabricated absence, silent fallbacks, differential drift, evidence honesty, provenance) + what NOT to flag + a reproduce-don't-trust method | Reference *(living)* |
| [`EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Efficiency-audit charter (sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`, for the EFFICIENCY lane) — two lanes kept separate: **A** dev/agent-loop cost (gate scoping, the re-bundle→ledger dance, redundant bundling, doc navigation) and **B** app runtime (super-linear DSP, redundant recompute, worker use, bundle/DOM cost). Measure-don't-guess at real full-night scale; correctness always wins; carries an "already-addressed / out-of-scope" list so the 2026-07-01 scoped-runs + reconcile-reporter work isn't re-filed | Reference *(living)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Privacy & security audit charter (third sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` / `EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) for a 100%-local, no-account health-signal tool — anchored to portable data-protection PRINCIPLES (minimization, storage-limitation, local control) for an **international** audience, **NOT** HIPAA/any single-nation regime. Flagship invariant: prove **NO-NETWORK egress**; also storage-minimization, export/PII leaks, untrusted-input→DOM (XSS), eval/CSP. Do-first = a two-layer `no-network` gate (static egress-grep + runtime throwing-trap probe) **with a required negative-control canary** — a planted-egress fixture the gate must catch — that promotes "100% local" from convention to enforced property | Reference *(living)* |
| [`MOBILE-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/MOBILE-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Mobile / small-screen behavior audit charter (fourth sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) — the apps ship a correct viewport + `@media` CSS so a phone is an intended surface. Bug-classes highest-yield first: **body horizontal overflow** (#1), **tap targets < 44×44**, **sub-12 px readable text**, viewport-meta/zoom-lock, sticky occlusion, breakpoint-collapse & re-bundle regressions, touch-only (`:hover`) affordances. Carries a **ready-to-run Playwright harness** (local http server + populate via each app's "Generate synthetic" button + the measure closure) so the setup isn't rediscovered; presentation-only + EXPORT-INERT; out-of-scope list (chart-tick micro-labels, the known Integrator nav dup) | Reference *(living)* |
| [`TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Test / CI audit charter (fifth sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) — audits the **tests and gates themselves**, the orthogonal axis: *does each gate actually fail when the code it guards breaks?* Method is **mutation, not reading** — break the guarded code, confirm the gate reds; a surviving mutation IS the finding. Gate-failure classes highest-yield first: **a gate that passes on a dead path** (#1 — the PPG `worker≡serial` that passed on a dead pool), **static-listed ≠ executed ≠ asserted**, **fake coverage** (testing a copy not the shipped code), **circular known-answers**, **unreal fixtures** (integer-`fs` that hid the fractional-index bug), **silent skips that shrink the gate**, **asserted-not-computed claims** (the stale "EXPORT-INERT" that shipped), **partition/shard holes**, **two lanes that diverge**. Out-of-scope list names the classes a META-GATE already enforces (`expected-skips.json`, `verify-shard-union`, `FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE`) so they aren't hand-re-filed — audit the meta-gate for holes instead. Cites the briefs each scar came from | Reference *(living)* |
| [`MOBILE-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md`](audits/MOBILE-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md) | **Report** — first run of the mobile charter (2026-07-15). **Layout is structurally sound: zero horizontal body-scroll on all 12 surfaces at 360/375/414, empty AND populated; viewport-meta correct on all 10; no mobile-only console errors; Integrator `.mobile-nav` dedupes to 1 at runtime.** Real defects are shared-class + fleet-wide: **F1** ~30–44 tap targets under 44×44 per analyzer (shared `.eb-btn` 104×32 · `.theme-toggle` 74×25 · `.synth-link` 124×18 · `.mode-btn` 48×22 · `select` h20 · `.sb-item` h38) — touch ergonomics; **F2** pervasive sub-12 px readable/finding/badge text (10–11.5 px) — legibility (global density, not a mobile regression); **F3 (low)** Integrator source triple `mobile-nav` (runtime-harmless). All CSS-only/EXPORT-INERT. Punch-list ends with "add a `browser-gates.mjs` 375 px no-overflow smoke". Coverage gaps: OxyDex populated + CPAPDex/DataUnifier/OverDex (file-only) not fully exercised | Findings *(report)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-01.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-01.md) | **Report** from running the privacy/security charter (2026-07-01). No-network invariant **HOLDS** (proven + now enforced by `no-network.html`). Demonstrated findings: **F1 (HIGH)** OxyDex stored-XSS via filename→`innerHTML`; **F2 (MED)** PulseDex reflected-XSS via comparison filename; **F4 (MED)** raw CSV + unscrubbed filename cached forever; **F5 (MED)** "Clear" doesn't clear + no complete "erase my data" path; **F7 (LOW–MED)** no CSP; **F3/F6 (LOW)**. Export posture already mature (not re-filed). Each finding → its own dated brief | Findings *(report)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-13.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-13.md) | **Report** — second run of the privacy/security charter (2026-07-13). **F1–F7 have held** (XSS removed/escaped, raw-CSV cache dropped, `dex-forget.js` erase-all inventory-gated, CSP + strict `script-src` on all 10 bundles, `no-network` gate now in CI). Three LOW / LOW–MED residues: **N1** the standalone analysis pages + `index.html` carry neither CSP nor no-network-gate coverage though they ingest recordings & persist checkpoints (which `dex-forget` already erases — a scope inconsistency); **N2** OxyDex `console.log`s the raw filename + raw CSV header bytes on every load (local-only, minimization); **N3** PpgDex's error toast is an unescaped `innerHTML` sink (F3-class, the node the escaper pass skipped). Each → its own dated brief | Findings *(report)* |
| [`no-network.html`](no-network.html) | **Gate** — the privacy analogue of `verify-provenance.html` (the charter's do-first). Origin-classified static egress grep of all 8 bundles + runtime throwing-trap boot + a **negative-control canary** (planted egress it must catch, benign local it must not) so a vacuous pass can't ship. Verdict via `window.__noNetworkOK`. Adds no app behavior (no re-bundle). **Follow-up:** wire into `tests/dex-tests.js` (both runners) | Gate |

## 1 · Cross-cutting contracts & specs (apply to every node)

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CLOCK-UNIFY-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CLOCK-UNIFY-BRIEF.md) | How the Clock Contract (floating wall-clock ms + `getUTC*`) was unified across apps | Spec / Brief |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-2026-06-29-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-2026-06-29-BRIEF.md) | The **Tepna Health Box** — a single bedside **Raspberry Pi** that auto-captures all 6 signals overnight, **serves** the bundled apps to the LAN (`http://tepna.local`, same-origin → shared profile/longitudinal store + runnable gates), and **stores** each night on an SSD (the durable source of truth). Three systemd roles (`tepna-capture` bleak supervisor · `tepna-web` static server · optional `tepna-analyze` nightly Node walk→compute→fusion). Per-device method (O2Ring onboard · H10 live PMD ECG mandatory · Verity onboard/PMD · Muse `muse-lsl`/`OpenMuse` for Athena · Lingo NOT raw → Health-export); TP-Link UB500 Plus / nRF52840 dongle bedside; emits the EXISTING vendor layouts so files route with NO new parser branch; Clock-Contract + device-id-filename companion-pairing contract; on-box privacy/backup-as-re-ingest ops. Hardware/daemon out-of-suite; the in-suite landing honors the gates | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-06-29)* |
| [`NODE-EXPORT-RECORDING-DURATION-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/NODE-EXPORT-RECORDING-DURATION-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | A defect **class** found by the 32-night full-fleet fuzz fold: a node whose `ganglior.node-export` declares **no recording-duration key** the Integrator reads (`durSec`/`durationMin`/`durationSec`/`endEpochMs`) is placed **only by its events**, so on an **event-sparse night** its window collapses to a zero-length point and it is **excluded from the fold**. Audits all 8 nodes (OxyDex·MotionDex·ECGDex ✓; **CPAPDex·PpgDex·PulseDex ✗**) and **fixes CPAPDex** (stamps `recording.durSec` = the session wall-clock span) — the second instance after the ECGDex one. PpgDex/PulseDex latent siblings + a PulseDex null-`t0` note are follow-ups. **CLOSED 2026-07-27:** both siblings declare a key the adapter reads (PpgDex `durSec`, PulseDex `durationMin`); the PulseDex null-`t0` suspicion is **REFUTED** — `parseRRInput` anchors a real 672 KB `Polar_H10_…_RR.txt` exactly (`t0Ms` → `2026-07-26T00:51:54`, 23655 stamps), so the observed null was the Clock Contract §2.6 refusing to fabricate, not an anchoring bug; and §4.3's class gate is built — **Every node declares a recording length the Integrator can read** (13 assertions, both lanes) brace-matches all 8 builders AND pins the five keys against `integrator-dsp.js` so it cannot silently under-test, mutation-checked by deleting MotionDex's `durSec`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-27)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-INTEGRATOR-FOLD-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-INTEGRATOR-FOLD-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | Folding the **real capture-host overnight recordings** through the **Integrator** headlessly (raw BLE → per-node `ganglior.node-export` → `runFusion`) across the whole roster (ECG·PPG·O2Ring·Motion·CPAP·Pulse). Records the fold method + findings (7/9 nights eligible; a 6-corner CPAP-device-AHI ↔ Integrator-confirmed-apnea cross-check that agrees; the 07-17/18 armband-PPG HRV divergence), and **fixes the two defects it exposed**: (1) `trio-batch.mjs` couldn't ingest the capture-host filename layout / per-night nesting; (2) ECGDex's export declared no recording duration, so an event-sparse ECG leg collapsed to a zero-length window and was excluded from the fold. Node-lane gated | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-24)* |
| [`HEALTH-BOX-VISION-2026-07-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/HEALTH-BOX-VISION-2026-07-01-BRIEF.md) | **Tepna Vigil** — the **product-vision layer** on top of `CAPTURE-HOST` (which owns the *how*). Names the bedside box (**Vigil** — watchful, nocturnal; a product-surface label only, touches NO frozen identifier), fixes the **phased capability model** (v1 capture/serve/store → next auto-analyze/context → someday automate), gives the **live multi-signal view** the hero framing (raw traces + derived-metric strip, same node DSP as the apps, badges carry over), specifies the **context/night-story** surface ("why was last night bad?" — room · weather · life on one clock; fetched context is host-side, NOT app egress), and records the experience principles. Styled mirror: `Tepna Vigil — Health Box Vision.html`. Vision brief; ships no code | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-01)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **What the first real multi-sensor bring-up surfaced** (`capture-host/`, out-of-suite, PR #129). Took the scaffold to **three sensors streaming live at once** (H10 ECG · Verity PPG · Wellue O2Ring-S SpO₂/pulse) and built the `HEALTH-BOX-VISION §4` hero live-view (served monitor: device bond/remember + stream-aware scope + averaged-beat/pulse/oximetry panels). Fixes a **~10 % HR bug** — `writers.py` emitted `timestamp [ms]` integer+absolute, so ECGDex inferred fs=143 not 130 (real PSL is relative+fractional). Vendor-format quirks found: **Verity PPG START needs CHANNELS as a u8** (else err 0x0B/0x05) and streams **delta/compressed frames** (new `_decode_delta`); the **O2Ring is an OxyII/T8520 device, NOT legacy Viatom** — advertises `S8-AW`, ignores the legacy service, speaks a separate `0xA5`-framed protocol on the `e8fb` service (new `oxyii.py`: XOR auth → poll `cmd=0x04`, no bond/no AES); the **H10 needs a bonded PMD link**; BlueZ needs a **connect lock** for concurrent devices. `CAPTURE-HOST` stays PROPOSED (no real overnight round-trip / `how-to-collect` notes / Pi bring-up yet) | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-16)* |
| [`MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **Scoping/agenda** — the derived values the Vigil multi-sensor capture (synchronized ECG · 4-ch PPG · dual ACC · GYRO/MAG · SpO₂ · RR) unlocks but the suite doesn't compute yet, each with its **published method source** + home node + evidence tier. Headline (Tier 1): **respiratory effort (chest ACC) → central-vs-obstructive apnea typing** (cross O2Ring desats) and **body position (chest-ACC gravity) → positional OSA**. Tier 2: cardiorespiratory/actigraphic **sleep staging**, fused **respiration rate**, **PAT** (not PTT), motion-gated cross-validated **HRV**. **Architectural blocker flagged:** ACC/GYRO/MAG have **no consumer node** yet (roster is single-signal) → needs a new motion/IMU node or Integrator ingestion. Compute in the apps only (never the box); each derivation becomes its own executable brief; extends `PAPERS-ROADMAP` §3. Cites Manoni'20, Ryser'22, Rocha'26, Fonseca'20, Schipper'24, Aktaruzzaman'17 et al. **Blocker RESOLVED 2026-07-17 (owner):** build a new motion node **MotionDex** → `MOTIONDEX-BUILD-2026-07-17`; the five IMU items route to future Integrator-fusion briefs that depend on it, PAT routes to the DONE `PAT-FEASIBILITY`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17 — agenda routed)* |
| [`MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **What executing four fusions (§1.1·§1.2·§2.2·§2.4) surfaced.** Headline: the SAME defect shape recurred three times — **fabricated absence in a per-epoch series** (a coverage gap read as a real measurement, then fed to a fraction): `actigraphy()` scored a zero-sample epoch as *immobile*, inflating a motion-gated HRV confidence; effort/posture gaps must read UNTYPED, never CENTRAL. `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` bug class #3 gained sub-class **3a** naming this variant; an audit pass over pre-existing series is still owed. Also: **PpgDex RIIV** is §2.2's missing third leg (`lombScargle` never tracks the HF peak — `TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION` §F1; the fuser is n-agnostic so fixing the DSP needs no Integrator change); a **gate that never drove the real ingest seam** let MotionDex ship unregistered in the Integrator; §2.1 staging is the last unexecuted derivation; and the `_norm` sweep is parked as a proven behaviour-inert no-op. | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-18)* |
| [`MOTIONDEX-BUILD-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MOTIONDEX-BUILD-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **Build brief for MotionDex — the fleet's motion / IMU node.** Spawned from `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS` after the owner chose a new node over Integrator ingestion. Ingests the Polar Sensor Logger IMU streams (Verity ACC/GYRO/MAG + H10 chest ACC) on the Clock Contract and computes the **single-signal** motion metrics — **body position** (gravity-vector, Rocha'26), **actigraphy/activity counts**, a **thoraco-abdominal effort waveform** (chest ACC, Ryser'22), and motion-artifact SQI (→ Ganglior `conf`) — exporting `ganglior.node-export` (`node:"MotionDex"`). Cross-sensor fusions (apnea typing, staging, motion-gated HRV) stay in the Integrator and consume this export, NOT here. ESM from birth (fan-out complete); owned plain-inline bundle; full gate sequence incl. an equiv leg on a committed synthetic IMU fixture. **Unblocks** the five `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS` Tier-1/2 fusion briefs. **EXECUTED 2026-07-18** — MotionDex ships as the fleet's 9th owned bundle; both gate lanes green (GATE A 9/9, browser `?full` 2770, equiv reproduces); roster named. Follow-ups → `MOTIONDEX-BUILD-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |
| [`MOTIONDEX-BUILD-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MOTIONDEX-BUILD-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **What shipping MotionDex surfaced.** None block the shipped node: (1) a render-coverage rig for `MotionDex.html` (render DOM path is Node-smoke-covered, not yet booted in an iframe); (2) body-position frame **calibration** to lift the posture label experimental→measured (Rocha'26 F1 needs a calibration step); (3) GYRO/MAGN are parsed but only counted — no consumer yet; (4) the equiv fixture reuses one input for both ACC slots (a distinct-chest twin would differentiate source selection); (5) the five `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS` Integrator-fusion briefs are now unblocked.  **CLOSED 2026-08-01 on its own bar** ("each item executed OR carrying an explicit park reason"). §4 executed 2026-07-21. **§1 PARKED with a reason about the WORK:** an iframe rig asserts only "it booted", and DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES §RN is the verdict on that class — 7 planted render defects (a ~325× mmol error, a hypoxic SpO₂ painted green, "well controlled" on AHI 40) all shipped green past exactly that coverage; 6 were closed NODE-side, 3 by extraction. MotionDex cannot take that route today because `motiondex-render.js` **exposes nothing** — a page-scoped IIFE whose only node-lane coverage is source-text scans — so closing §1 properly means hoisting the KPI arithmetic to exposed pure functions FIRST, then deciding whether a rig adds anything. Re-open as an extraction item, not a rig item. **§5 agenda CORRECTED:** apnea typing was executed and then WITHDRAWN (effort during central apnea is 0.99× its own baseline; AUC 0.691), so a reader working the list top-down would have rebuilt a removed foundation — the effort series itself stands | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`DOCS-LEDGER-CHECK3B-BLIND-ROW-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DOCS-LEDGER-CHECK3B-BLIND-ROW-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — the gate that reported "in sync" about rows it never read.** `check3b` skipped any DOCS-INDEX row carrying no status marker (`if (!m) return;`) and then declared the whole set in sync, so deleting a status cell left every gate green — which is how it was found, via a shell-quoting slip that ate a cell. Re-measuring split §2’s 48 blind rows into **36 genuinely status-less** and **12 that stated a status the regex could not see** (`*(**DONE 2026-07-14**)*`, `*(✅ DONE …)*`, `(**DONE …**)`) — a **second defect the brief did not identify**, and rows that had data to compare and were never compared. Recovering them surfaced **0** new mismatches, so the index was honest, but the gate could not have known that. Took **option (1)** rather than the recommended dated cutoff: the quiet window existed (one open PR) and every status is read from the brief header **by script**, which §4 requires and which beats transcribing 36 by hand. Matcher loosened, 36 rows backfilled, and a missing marker is now its own failure so the message names which of the two things broke. **Mutation-verified both ways** — deleting a cell reds the presence check *while the equality check still says "in sync"*, which is the original bug visible in one run; a wrong status reds equality and leaves presence green. Now total over all **219** single-brief rows with an executable status — no cutoff, no grandfather list — partial only where it is structural (multi-brief rows, `REFERENCE`/`CHECKPOINT`). **§4a's sibling blind spot BUILT 2026-08-01 as `check3c` (§4a.1)** — within one brief, a section must not be claimed both closed and still open (§4a records **three instances in `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` alone, in one day**, one of which "nearly redid" a session's work). §4a declined to build it because *"a gate that cries wolf on the legitimate partial case would be turned off, which is worse than the hole"*, so the cry-wolf rate was **measured first**: 12 briefs carry a `Still open:` line, **0** contradict themselves — it ships as a regression guard with no live subject and says so. Two refinements were forced by real false positives during that measurement: section ids keep **letter suffixes** (`PROFILED-HOTSPOTS` closes §1a while §1b is open) and the partial-vocabulary veto is judged **per section id in its own ±30-char window** (`OXYDEX-PB-OVERCALL`'s "Still open: §4 item 3 only" is §4a's legitimate case, and a line-wide veto would excuse an entire single-line header on one stray "half" — where all three historical instances lived). **Both wrong capture bounds looked like success**: to the first `.` truncates inside "§1.4" and yields zero false positives *and* zero true positives; to end-of-line sweeps in five unrelated sections. **And the gate shipped as a no-op once, green** — an early revision scanned `briefSet`, a name→`1` existence map rather than the brief text, so it matched nothing and passed; the same hollow shape §1 is about, inside the fix for it, caught by planting the real §1.7 contradiction and watching the gate stay green. Mutation-verified both directions plus four permanent self-tests so the matcher cannot rot while unexercised. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`APNEA-TYPING-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/APNEA-TYPING-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Respiratory effort × desaturation → central-vs-obstructive apnea typing** — the first of MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS's five now-unblocked Integrator fusions (executes §1.1). Answers the one thing oximetry alone can't: a desat with **effort present through it** (MotionDex chest-ACC) is **obstructive**, effort-absent is **central**. Two atomic phases: (1) MotionDex exports an additive coverage-honest per-epoch **effort series** (`present:null` when chest-ACC isn't recording — never fabricated-absent, the ×0.72 coverage artifact one modality over); (2) the Integrator ingests it + types each OxyDex desat over its window (reusing the P7 window/coverage machinery), emitting `apnea_obstructive`/`apnea_central` events + an additive `apneaTyping` split beside the unchanged AHI. **experimental** tier (3-device, Clock-Contract-aligned, surrogate effort, not diagnostic; distinct from CPAPDex's device-scored CSL index). Cites Manoni'20, Ryser'22. **EXECUTED 2026-07-18** — Phase 1 (MotionDex effort series, PR #171) + Phase 2 (Integrator `typeApneaByEffort` + additive `summary.apneaTyping`); gated by the `apnea-typing` group (effort-through⇒obstructive · flat⇒central · no-coverage⇒untyped-never-central · no-MotionDex⇒null). Export-field-only, per the P7 `apneaCoupling` precedent. ⚠️ **Phase 2's TYPE was WITHDRAWN 2026-07-31** by `INTEGRATOR-APNEA-TYPING-REVIEW-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md` — measurement showed the effort feature does not separate the two classes, so the `apnea-typing` group now pins the **negative** and `obstructive`/`central` are `null`. **Phase 1 (the effort series) stands unchanged**; the `experimental` tier and the proposed UI/EVENT-LEXICON follow-ups are void. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **Findings + concerns from the second bring-up** (out-of-suite, `645810d` on `claude/vigil-capture-parity` + uncommitted clock work). Full multi-stream capture (Verity PPG **4-ch** + **ACC/GYRO/MAG**, H10 **ACC**+**RR**, O2Ring motion/battery) via **PMD settings negotiation** + STOP-before-START + one multiplexed SSE; Tepna-unified monitor; **clock/NTP/timezone control** + **CLOCK_MONOTONIC-anchored** capture stamps. **Fixed 2026-07-18:** empty-vendor artifact (identity now single-sourced + gated in BOTH the daemon and the Remember API), the DST re-anchor (zone-keyed, so a fall-back night stays monotonic), vestigial `incoming_subdir` (dropped); writer flush+fsync confirmed done. **Unvalidated:** GYRO/MAG/PPI decoders vs a PSL export (only delta path / motion-response seen), ACC frame-type, monotonic re-anchor vs a real NTP step, the sudo clock-apply path on a real box. **Deferred:** per-night clock provenance, `offsetMin` in exports, monitor lite-mode, and a **multi-sensor DERIVATIONS** agenda (respiratory-effort→central/obstructive apnea · body-position→positional OSA · PTT · motion-gated HRV · actigraphy) that should become its own brief | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-16)* |
| [`VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Three operational guardrails so the bedside box stops *silently* losing a night** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage). (1) **Storage guard** (`diskguard.py` + `storage_poller`): free-space in `status.json`, **opt-in** age retention (`keep_nights: 0` = never delete; matches only the strict `YYYY-MM-DD` night dirs so `incoming/`/`stored/` are safe; tonight always protected), low-disk **alert** (never auto-eats recent data). (2) **Push alerts** (`alerts.py` + `alert_poller`): a generic webhook (ntfy/Discord/Slack/HA — no vendor baked in), **disabled by default**, **edge-triggered** so a flapping sensor can't spam — fires on a sensor offline past `offline_sec`, its recovery, daemon (re)start, and low disk; every webhook failure swallowed (a miss is never a missed night). (3) **systemd watchdog** (`sdnotify.py` + `sd_watchdog`, `Type=notify`+`WatchdogSec=120`): pure-stdlib READY/WATCHDOG/STOPPING heartbeat at half the interval turns the box's signature **hung-but-alive** wedge into an automatic restart (`Restart=always` never fires on a wedge). New config is deploy-only (kept OUT of `settings_schema`, same as `root`/`adapter`). Verified end-to-end against a real `AF_UNIX` socket, a real aiohttp webhook round-trip, and real `disk_report`. Follow-ups: per-night QC summary · automated offload · monitor UI for `storage` · web-control auth | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Executes four of the parent's follow-ups** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage, 2942 stmts / 800 tests). (1) **Per-night QC summary** (`nightqc.py` + `qc_poller`): `summarize(night_dir, devices)` rolls a night up against the configured devices — rows per device × declared stream (newlines − 1, one header per `writers` file), a stream with **zero rows** is `missing` (the header-only file a rejected PMD START / never-worn strap leaves), `ok` only when every stream produced data; pure + cheap (parses filenames, counts newlines in 1 MiB chunks — a multi-GB ECG never loaded whole); writes `<night>/QC-SUMMARY.json` + `status.json.qc`, read-only, errors swallowed. (2) **Bad-night alert**: `qc_poller` fires **once per night** via the guardrails webhook when a stream is still empty past `qc.alert_after_sec` (the grace kills false alarms on a just-started night). (3) **Web-control auth** (`webmon` middleware): optional `web.token` gates every **POST** control action (bond/forget/remember/pull/settings/clock); **GET reads stay open**; `Authorization: Bearer` or `X-Tepna-Token`, constant-time compare; off by default. (4) **Night offload** (`nightarchive.py` + `archive_poller`): **mirrors** each completed night (never tonight) to a configured `dest` (NAS / served dir / backup) — idempotent + resumable via a `.archived` marker, **never moves** the source (retention prunes it separately). Verified against **real** `StreamWriter`/`Spo2CsvWriter` output. Remaining: monitor UI for `qc`/`storage` (frontend) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Makes the box recover a link that is UP but carrying no data** — the failure class the night guardrails could see but not act on (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage, 835 tests). Triggered by a real loss: 2026-07-19 the H10's PMD START was answered **`already_streaming` (0x06)** — `is_started()` rightly reads that as live — and **ECG + ACC sat at ZERO ROWS for ten minutes** behind a green link while HR/RR flowed; the hold loop ran on `client.is_connected` alone so it had no reason to end. Root cause is upstream (`polar-ble-sdk#287`): the H10 serves **one** PMD stream and does not release it when a client dies without a clean disconnect, so the ACK belongs to a dead subscriber. (1) **Stall watchdog** (`stream_is_stalled`, all three runners): watches **bytes reaching a file**, not ACKs — 90 s of silence ends the session, which frees the stream and re-runs the negotiation (the manual `bluetoothctl disconnect` fix, automated); the ring watches **decoded frames, not rows**, so an unworn ring is provably not torn down. (2) **`already_streaming` challenged** with a forced STOP + re-START (the pre-existing STOP never read its ack). (3) **`pmd.NO_ACK`**: a dropped control indication is no longer filed as "unsupported settings" — it used to delete the writer, so one lost indication cost that stream the session and a failed control subscribe cost **all** of them. (4) **Every BLE await bounded** — `_connect` held the **process-global** `_CONNECT_LOCK` across an unbounded `connect()`; `PolarPsFtp.__aexit__` ran unbounded *inside* the caller's cancellation so `wait_for` could never return; `polar_offline_op` acquired the lock *outside* its timeout; `pull_oxyii_session` had **neither**. (5) **Supervised tasks** (`keep_running`/`supervise`): `main()` never gathers until `_STOP`, so an escaping exception retired a runner or watchdog **silently**, traceback never retrieved. (6) **Clock re-sync converges** (`clock_resync_reason`): `adrift` fires on absolute skew and the re-baseline erases the memory of trying, so a constant non-zero offset (e.g. Verity ~4 h) can never converge — **latent, not observed at scale**: the full 7 h night of 2026-07-19 shows the trigger firing **once** (−3.0 s) and converging, and the brief's original "~15% of every night, observed live" claim is **corrected in-place** as inference stated as evidence. Now gives up after 3 proven-useless attempts and reports `clock_uncorrectable`, while a real **jump** still re-syncs. (7) **Ring/legacy parity**: header-only file cleanup, `run_viatom` honours `_RECOVER`/`_OXYII_PAUSE` and no longer skips `START_CMD` silently. Follow-ups: `sd_watchdog` proves loop liveness not capture liveness · `classify_adapter_health` under-detects (left alone deliberately — it drives the adapter power-cycle) · `run_muse` returncode + orphaned child | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes three of the parent's open items, all found by RUNNING the daemon and measuring it** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`oxyii.py`/`webmon.py`, 850 tests). (1) **The daemon could not be stopped** — measured 2026-07-20, `SIGTERM` left it alive past **101 s** with nothing in the log, which under systemd is a `systemctl restart` hanging to `TimeoutStopSec` then a SIGKILL mid-write. **It was never a BLE problem**: bounding each shutdown phase and naming the offender showed `gather()` completing cleanly and `AppRunner.cleanup()` never returning — `webmon.stream`'s SSE live-view is a `while True` that ends only when the CLIENT goes away, so an open browser tab blocked the restart. Fixed at source (an `on_shutdown` hook that sets a flag **and pushes a sentinel**, since the handler is parked in `q.get()`) plus a bounded backstop that NAMES what refused to stop (`asyncio.wait`, not `wait_for(gather)` — the latter cancels the children again so the stuck tasks finish before they can be named). Verified live with a real SSE client attached: **2 s**. (2) **`run_muse`** — `CancelledError` is a `BaseException`, so on shutdown `terminate()` was skipped entirely, orphaning `muselsl` holding the Muse's BLE link so the NEXT start could not connect; teardown moved to a `finally` with terminate→wait→kill, and `connected=True` is no longer set BEFORE the child exists (a tool dying on its first line showed a green card all night while respawning every 5 s). (3) **`oxyii.Reassembler`** bounds a declared length (`MAX_FRAME_LEN`, deliberately loose at 2048 so a future 517 MTU cannot break the `.dat` pull) — a 16-bit `ln` could claim 65535 and swallow ~7 min of valid frames. (4) **O2Ring RTC investigated and deliberately NOT changed**: measured against the real pulled session, the device-stamped start is within **2 s** over 9 h 09 m, unaided (its first sync came 1 h 47 m later) — contradicting the `~+151 s` drift note. Documents the one real flaw left alone by owner decision: the `new recording session` sync fires ~1 s AFTER the header is stamped, so it can only help the next session. Still open: `sd_watchdog` liveness · `classify_adapter_health` · `pull_session` internals · startup ladder · O2Ring reconnect churn (59 link errors in 7 h) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-DEEP-ANALYSIS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-DEEP-ANALYSIS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **Multi-agent deep analysis of the Vigil appliance — code audit + detector/BLE/hardware research + adversarial verification** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). 5 code auditors + 5 research agents → an adversarial verifier that **rejected 22 of 40 proposals**; every HIGH finding then **re-verified against source by the author** (the verifier ran with its safety classifier down). **Top 5 (all source-confirmed):** (1) **post-connect GATT setup awaits are UNBOUNDED** in all three runners (`capture.py:762`+7) though the file states "EVERY BLE await must be bounded" (`:126`) — `run_oxyii` sets `connected=True` at `:1204` then does unbounded `start_notify`/auth-writes at `:1347-1368`, so a BlueZ wedge after `connect()` freezes a device at `connected=True` invisible to every watchdog; (2) **the live R-peak/pulse detectors threshold on the WINDOW MAX** (`detectRs thr=0.55·amax` `:1375`, `detectPulses thr=0.4·smax` `:1501`) so one motion spike blinds HR for the whole 5–7 s window; (3) **adapter pin + RSSI hang off deprecated `hcitool`** (`link_rssi.py:72`, absent on Pi 5 → the deaf-onboard-radio mis-pin), while `bonding.py:106` already pins by MAC over `bluetoothctl`; (4) **`bond()` leaves every sensor `Trusted`** (`bonding.py:190`, no `untrust` in-tree) — the kernel's own reconnect races bleak for the ACL slot (the recorded `br-connection-canceled`); (5) **the byte-stall watchdog is collective, not per-stream** (`capture.py:907`) so a dead stream behind a live sibling is never caught. Plus a **GET `/api/polar/recordings` that bypasses the POST-only token gate while pausing capture** (`webmon.py:471/:43`), a **bluetoothctl newline-injection** via unvalidated device address, and **`_save()` swallowing config-write errors** (`ok:true` on a full disk). §3 recommends better detectors with papers — **live:** IIR-bandpass + Elgendi/TERMA and **MSPTDfast** for PPG (Charlton 2022/2025); **ECGDex disk leg:** move off classic Pan-Tompkins (Porr 2024: JF 75.3) to **EngZee** (clean-room, GPL) or **Pan-Tompkins++** (MIT port) + sub-sample interpolation + a **skewness/Orphanidou SQI gate** — gate any switch on the real 20-night corpus, not MIT-BIH. §4 ranks 11 BLE fixes (bound awaits · D-Bus resolve · untrust · per-stream stall · scan-inside-lock · cache-invalidation · recovery ladder · clean-exit give-up) and confirms **MTU=23 is a bleak placeholder, not a fault**. §5 confirms the minimal BOM (Pi 5 + NVMe + UB500 on a bedside extension; nRF52840 only past ~4 links). §7 records what's already shipped so it isn't re-proposed; §8 is a 7-phase plan. Regression surface = `capture-host/tests` (pytest), no Dex gate. | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-22)* |
| [`VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Field-observation record of a running box, watched live 2026-07-19 21:35 → 2026-07-20 10:14** — a full 7.37 h night plus the idle morning after (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). Records ONLY what was measured on the running system, separated from inference, each item with a status. **Healthy:** three independent chains agree on HR (ECG 53–55 · H10 device 53 · O2Ring PR 53–55 bpm), rates match negotiation (ECG 129.94/130, O2Ring PPG 125.57/125.738), ACC scale 997 mg vs 1000, **no intra-segment sample loss** (largest gap = one sample period on every stream), stratum-1 PPS clock, device skew ≤ 0.25 s, SpO₂ median 96 % with 0.5 % < 90 %. **Night yield:** Verity ~100 % · H10 96.8 % · O2Ring **87.8 %** across **115** session files. **E1** live link carrying no data (`already_streaming`, 9.7 min of ECG+ACC lost at 21:36) — FIXED/merged by #280, but **still unvalidated against a real night** (it started 04:55, after the night ended). **E2** the daemon **does not stop on SIGTERM** — reproduced live: handler registered (`SigCgt` bit 15), signal consumed (`SigPnd: 0`), yet no `shutdown: stopping` line ever logged, files still growing 3 min later, SIGINT equally ignored, **SIGKILL required**; fix `f43122b` sits UNMERGED on `claude/vigil-shutdown`. **E3** O2Ring **reconnect storm** — 178 reconnects, 115 fragments, 12 % of the night lost across 10 gaps > 60 s (worst 245 s), 10 watchdog wedge signs + 1 adapter power-cycle that DID recover it; the E1 stall watchdog does not address link churn. **E4** nothing gates capture on **wear state** — an unworn Verity on a desk wrote **453 MB in 4.16 h** (ACC at 416 Hz = 71 %); the O2Ring/H10 report `worn`, the Verity reports `null`. **E5** `LINK.csv` under-reports dropouts (25 s poll steps over ~12 s drops; `connected=1` across three real reconnects). **E6** disk 21.31 → 19.71 GB with `keep_nights: 0` and no `alerts:` block — both guardrails inert. **E7** Verity ACC/GYRO run 2.2 % below nominal (406.73/416, 50.83/52) — device clock, not a decode error. **E8** phone timestamps non-monotonic up to 470 ms while sensor timestamps stay strictly monotonic — parsers must order on the sensor column. Proposes: merge the shutdown fix · validate E1 across a real night · a brief for the O2Ring churn · wear-gating + Verity ACC rate · switch the alert/retention config on | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`VIGIL-WEAR-GATE-AND-ACC-CAP-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-WEAR-GATE-AND-ACC-CAP-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes E4** of VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). **(1) ACC rate cap — KEPT:** Verity ACC 416→52 Hz, an 8x cut on 71% of the box's bytes at **no cost** (MotionDex reads `fs` from the data; widest band is respiratory 0.1-0.6 Hz), verified live. **(2) Motion wear-gate — DISPROVEN & REVERTED:** validated against real worn-night data and FAILED — a worn Verity on the **left ankle** during still sleep reads **~1 mg |acc| std, BELOW a desk's ~2.3 mg**, so no threshold separates worn from off-body and the gate would have paused writing through real sleep. Shipped off-by-default (PR #297), disproven, code/config/tests removed. The only sound Verity wear signal is its **PPG pulse**, not ACC — a separate future feature. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-RECONNECT-BACKOFF-AND-LINK-COUNT-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-RECONNECT-BACKOFF-AND-LINK-COUNT-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes E3 + E5** of VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS — both the same root class as the E1 stall bug: "connected" is not "working" (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`writers.py`, 866 tests). **E3** (reconnect storm — 178 reconnects, 115 session files, 12% lost in one night): the dominant error is `failed to discover services, device disconnected` (38x — connect succeeds then drops during GATT discovery), and reconnects were evenly spaced at ~21 s, NOT backing off. Cause: `run_oxyii` reset `backoff=5` on bare connect, before the session proved viable, so every doomed attempt reset it. Fix: reset backoff only when DATA flows — a connect-then-drop now backs off 5→10→…→60, a streaming ring recovers fast. Reduces self-inflicted churn (fragmentation, adapter contention, battery); the discovery-drop root cause is signal/hardware, out of code scope. **E5** (LINK.csv under-reported dropouts — the 25 s `rssi_poller` reads connected=1 at both ends of a sub-25 s drop): `_set` now counts the False→True reconnect edges into a per-device `link_epoch`, written as an appended-last LINK.csv column, so a dropout the poll sampled over is still counted — the sidecar becomes authoritative for the NUMBER of drops (exact timing stays in session-file boundaries). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-O2RING-AUTOPULL-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-O2RING-AUTOPULL-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Auto-pull the O2Ring's onboard `.dat` so a night's SpO2 never depends on the live BLE link** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`pull_session.py`, 870 tests). The 2026-07-20 night (box in another room, O2Ring at −85 dBm, connect-stream-dropped 367x → 70% live coverage) showed the onboard recording is the real full-night SpO2 — but only if the `.dat` reaches the box. (1) **Idempotent pull**: `_pull_once` skips a session already on disk at the device-reported size, so `which=all` no longer re-downloads the whole flash each cycle. (2) **`autopull_poller`** (opt-in `pull.auto`): pulls `which=all` only when the ring is OFF the finger (never interrupts a live sleep), **retries up to `auto_retries` (3) per cycle to DRAIN the small FIFO flash** before a missed session is overwritten, best-effort on an unreachable ring / busy slot, reuses the bounded connect-locked `pull_oxyii_session`. Prerequisite (operator): the ring must actually record the night to flash. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21)* |
| [`VIGIL-ADAPTER-FALSE-WEDGE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-ADAPTER-FALSE-WEDGE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **A real wedge trace for the deferred `classify_adapter_health` item** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`, 861 tests). First evening on 4 devices, the adapter watchdog turned a harmless BLE hiccup into a **~25 min self-inflicted outage**: the churny O2Ring (E3) threw `InProgress` 22x while the H10 streamed 75k ECG samples cleanly, the watchdog read that lone InProgress as an **adapter** wedge and power-cycled the whole radio **8x in 18 min** (each cycle dropping all 4 links), gave up at max-cycles, and recovered **1 min after the power-cycling STOPPED** — the cycles were the outage, not the cure. Fix: `InProgress` counts toward a wedge **only when the radio serves nobody** (`not any_connected`) — a lone InProgress while another device is live is benign device contention, not an adapter wedge. Phantom-link signal unchanged (real-wedge detection preserved: all-down + InProgress still flags). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-OFFLOAD-AND-RETENTION-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OFFLOAD-AND-RETENTION-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Settles how finished nights leave the capture box and how it reclaims space** (executes E6 of `VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS`; deploy config, no bundle/DSP change). The box writes ~1.5 GB/night at `keep_nights: 0` (never prune), so a 240 GB SSD fills in **~3–4 months** → silent capture failure (a full FS makes fsync fail while the daemon looks alive). **Decision, recorded so it is not re-litigated:** a manual monthly copy-then-wipe is REJECTED — it re-opens a ~29-day single-copy window (a sudden SSD death loses ~28 unrecoverable nights), is a human step that gets skipped, and is not cheaper in money (same NAS either way). **Adopted** (both halves already coded): (1) **nightly mirror** (`nightarchive.py` + `archive_poller`) mirrors each COMPLETED night to `archive.dest` — idempotent/resumable, MIRRORS never moves; (2) **retention** `keep_nights: 14` prunes only the LOCAL copy of old strict-`YYYY-MM-DD` dirs, tonight always protected, reclaimed only after it is safely on the NAS. Net: every night on two disks within ~24 h, box self-manages space, zero recurring manual step, ~2-week local window preserved for trend work. **Depends on PR #292** (archive/QC/prune moved to `asyncio.to_thread`) — do NOT enable `archive.enabled` on a build predating it, or a slow/hung dest starves capture and trips the watchdog. **Transport (decide with the hardware, safest-first):** (1) NAS PULLS via a TrueNAS `192.168.0.142` Rsync-over-SSH task (nothing mounted on the recorder — a NAS outage is invisible; SMB 445 open / NFS 2049 closed as probed); (2) local big disk (Pi/N100/800-G3-mini + 2 TB NVMe → a local copy, no mount); (3) box PUSHES to a CIFS mount (last resort — couples recorder health to the NAS). Open: production box choice (HP 800 G3 mini ~$135 vs fanless MINIX Z100-0dB ~$345) · transport → fill `dest` · root-level setup (SSH key or cifs-utils+fstab) needs owner go-ahead · confirm real SSD/NVMe not eMMC/SD | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Executes a 3-pass audit of `capture-host/`** (out-of-suite, Python, test-first → **100% pytest**, 3293 stmts / 890 tests, `ruff E9,F` clean). **#1 (night boundary):** `writers.night_dir` rolls a folder by session START, so a capture crossing midnight keeps writing to its start-date folder — but `qc_poller`/`archive_poller`/retention keyed on `_now()`'s date, so at 00:00 they treated the LIVE night as yesterday: QC summarised an empty new folder and (with `archive.enabled`) the archiver could mirror-and-mark the in-progress night MID-RECORDING, truncating it. New **`diskguard.active_nights(captures, settle_sec)`** (nights with a write inside `storage.settle_sec`, default 20 min) is now the anchor — QC reads the active night, archive mirrors only settled nights and protects every active one (a cross-midnight reconnect leaves two), retention protects all active; the `LINK`/`CLOCK` sidecars (which appended to the boot night's folder FOREVER) now roll per calendar day. **#2 (false confidence):** `nightqc` flagged only ZERO-row streams as missing, so a 40%-of-rate trickle (Verity IMU; a stream that died at hour one) read green — `summarize` now estimates per-stream **coverage** (rows vs configured/nominal rate × span-from-mtimes), flags <50% as `degraded`, `ok` requires no gap AND no trickle; the monitor + web surface show it and `link_epoch` (the E5 reconnect count a green dot hides). **#3** `run_polar` reset backoff on a bare connect (E3 parity fix → reset on viable data). **#4** one runner per BLE link — `register_runner`/`unregister_runner` dedupe a re-Remember and stop the runner on Forget (was: two runners racing one link / an orphan reconnecting a dropped device). **#14** `run_viatom` honours `_OXYII_PAUSE` mid-session. **#8** archive skips a dest whose backup volume is unmounted instead of makedirs-ing the tree onto the boot disk. **#9/#10** a `STATUS[host]` block surfaces `started_at` (spurious-restart signal) + adapter mis-pin. **#7/#11/#12/#13/#15/#16** stale MTU comments, `/opt` layout, `stream.stall_sec` wiring, `provenance_poll_sec` + `time`/`stream`/`power`/`o2ring` config docs, dead `describe()` param. **Declined, recorded:** per-stream stall TEARDOWN (#5 — would trade a working ECG for a re-negotiation gamble on the H10's shared link; visibility via #2 is the right answer) and removing the dead `frames_dropped`/`frames_duplicated` LINK.csv columns (#6 — the positional contract put `link_epoch` last on purpose; removing middles would shift it). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **What the FIRST real overnight on the shipped code exposed — the coverage feature (parent #2) was right in intent but wrong in two edge cases only a real cross-midnight night could show** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest). **Validated as working:** #1 midnight-anchor (session ran 19:46→04:41, no truncation, `active_nights` protected the live night across 00:00) and O2Ring **auto-pull** (fired 04:52 off-finger, pulled the complete onboard `.dat` — 8.9 h / ~1 Hz, `declared_size` matched — backing the churny live link's ~79%). **§1 intermittent-day span inflation:** the box also ran daytime 07-21, so coverage measured each stream against the folder's ~20 h mtime span and read a live stream as **0% degraded** (H10 ECG was growing 657 lines/3 s). Fixed by scoping coverage to the CURRENT session (group by `_YYYYMMDDHHMMSS_` start stamp; a >`_SESSION_GAP_SEC` (1 h) gap starts a new one) → span 19.9 h → 37 min, coverage 0-18% → 86-101%. **§2 cross-midnight:** (a) `night_dir` rolls each connection into a folder by START date, so an overnight begun before midnight lives in TWO date folders and QC read only one → pool the previous-day folder when the current session opened just after midnight (gated so a mid-day session never re-reads a prior day); (b) clustering by start-STAMP dropped a stable long connection (the H10's one 19:46→02:41 stamp) as a "gap" → **merge ACTIVE INTERVALS `[start, last-write]`** instead. Live: span 5.4 h → **9.3 h** (full overnight), H10 34% → **93-96%**, `degraded` []. **Lesson:** 100% line coverage passed while the metric was wrong for its PRIMARY case (every real overnight) — a synthetic twin with fresh mtimes never crosses midnight; both bugs were found by running `summarize` against the live folder, not by re-reading the tests. Open: nothing (parent's declined #5/#6 + off-by-default alerts remain owner-decision). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`VIGIL-OVERNIGHT-FINDINGS-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OVERNIGHT-FINDINGS-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | **What a second real overnight (2026-07-23→24), observed live in 13 passes, exposed — and the root cause static analysis missed** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). **The night:** the bedside USB dongle (TP-Link **UB500, RTL8761B**, `hci0`) **wedged twice** (01:39–02:16, 03:33–~04:50 — ~110 min all-device data loss), each ended only by a lucky firmware self-heal while a healthy internal radio sat idle. **Root cause (below the app, found in `/sys`):** USB **autosuspend** on the RTL8761B (`power/control=auto`) — a documented firmware-hang trigger; the internal radio was owner-tested worse for bedside range, so the dongle stays. **Watchdog was lying:** a DOWN dongle fails connects with a plain `TimeoutError` (neither `InProgress` nor phantom-link), so `classify_adapter_health` read a dead radio as "not worn" and logged `adapter healthy again` **25×+ over a DOWN adapter**, resetting its escalation counter and delaying the (unprivileged, `exited 1`) recovery ladder ~65 min. **Fixes shipped this session:** (a) **#398** — `classify_adapter_health(adapter_up=…)` + bounded `_adapter_is_up(hci)` catch a DOWN pinned adapter directly (param added LAST+optional, guarded by `not any_connected` so a live link/misread never power-cycles a working radio); (b) **#399** — suppress the `InProgress` wedge inference when the adapter is CONFIRMED up (fixes a live false power-cycle at 09:46 that would drop live links on a worn night); (c) **`systemd/50-tepna-btdongle.rules`** — udev rule pinning `power/control=on` (the actual PREVENTION; one-time root install) + config `watchdog.usb_path: 11-1.2`. **Also:** the O2Ring onboard `.dat` auto-pull **rescued the whole SpO₂ night** (7 h continuous, OxyDex: meanSpO₂ 96.8 %, ODI 7.1/h, AHI-est 3.2 = normal) that the live link had shredded to 0.24 coverage / 189 fragments — vindicating the E3 backup; but the **Polars have no equivalent** (Polar SDK: online streaming and offline recording are mutually exclusive per data type, HR excepted), so their two wedge gaps are unrecoverable — owner chose live-first (keep the waveform, fix the radio). Prioritised plan P0–P4; 942 pytest green. Follow-ups: grant the recovery ladder `CAP_NET_ADMIN` (§P1.2) · reconnect backoff + file-set resume (§P2) · retention-gate-on-archive (§P3.2). | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-24)* |
| [`CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **Auto-harvest the ResMed card off an ez Share Wi-Fi SD adapter — the one signal with no capture automation** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Executes the route `how-to-collect/cpap-edf.md §Capture B` already promised. CPAP joins Tepna as **files, not a BLE stream** (`CPAPDEX-PHASE9-FOLLOWUPS §2`), so this is a daily file harvester (`cpap_harvest.py` + `cpap_poller`), not a device runner: no BLE surface, no adapter, no connect lock, and its **own** `/api/state` block and monitor card rather than a pseudo-device that would render as a permanently-dead sensor. **Protocol confirmed by reading a working implementation, not documentation** — `GET /dir?dir=A:` returns HTML, downloads follow the listing's `href` (`download?file=<8.3path>`, NOT the `fname=`/`fdir=` form in the 2021 notes); row metadata **precedes** the anchor, so anchor-first parsing shifts every file's size/time by one and looks entirely plausible. `STR.EDF` must land as `STR.edf` or the night has session EDFs and no summary. **Schedule is 13:00, not the 09:00 first proposed** — measured across the 14 most recent nights, last-write ran 08:35→12:02, median 08:56, with **6/14 still writing after 09:00**, and the late files are the big ones (on `20260725` CSL/EVE landed 03:50/06:42 but PLD, SA2 and **BRP — the 25 Hz flow waveform** — all at 10:10; a 09:00 pull would have taken two small files, missed the waveform, and **reported success**). Two operator guarantees are **enforced, not assumed**: `harden_profile()` forces `ipv4.never-default`/`ignore-auto-dns` every run and `wifi_up()` verifies the default route did not move to the card, and the association is released in a `finally`, again under `asyncio.shield` on task exit, and on entry to clear a run that died mid-transfer. Phase 0 executed on real hardware: AirSense 11 AutoSet, **197 nights on the card**, ~2.5 MB/night, 130 KB/s → a night ≈ 21 s, seven ≈ 148 s, full backfill ≈ 65 min; every file round-trips through `cpapdex-edf.js` `readEDF` (0 problems), Clock Contract confirmed (`offsetMin` null). **Unplanned finding:** `SA2.edf` carries `SpO2.1s`+`Pulse.1s` — a second, wired, drop-free SpO₂ source over the identical interval, worth its own brief given the ring spends 17% of nights below −85 dBm | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-28 — all four phases shipped; the harvest runs unattended and the box holds 197 nights / 529 MB. Followed by `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS`; the card itself is written up in `EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION`)* |
| [`CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **The residue of executing `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST`** — the harvest now runs unattended at 13:00 and the box holds 197 nights / 529 MB in a clean steady state, so this carries what execution *taught* and what it did not close. **§1** the lessons: the completeness MODEL was the bug, not the transport (five days of flaky-card symptoms, every one of them `ceil` meeting a symmetric tolerance — five identical fetches would have ended it on day one); **a promise in a docstring is not a behaviour** (`cpap_poller` documented the zero-file alert, logged a warning, and published `ok` — the write-up is what made it look handled); verifying a privilege question **outside** the sandbox that constrains the daemon proves nothing (`/tmp` worked over SSH, failed under `ProtectSystem=strict`); and a claim of absence must be checked against a **total**, never a sorted excerpt (a 179 MB ECG file hidden above the tail of an `ls | sort | tail` became a false report of 6 h lost). **§2** open: the CPAP is recording **its own oximetry** — `SA2.edf` carries `SpO2.1s` + `Pulse.1s` at 1 Hz every night, a second wired drop-free SpO2 source over the identical interval, unclaimed, and exactly the gap-filler for the 17 % of nights the O2Ring spends below -85 dBm; **no failure path has ever fired in the field** (all unit tested, the alert has never delivered a real webhook — worth one deliberate fault injection); two non-comparable throughput measurements. **§3** deliberately not done: station mode is unconfirmable over HTTP (219-byte catch-all `200`) and moot; a second NIC is unnecessary on a wired box. **§4** carries the **four `DEEP-AUDIT-III` punch-list items nothing else owns** — §3.6 (Integrator publishes an ECG-only number under a note saying otherwise) and MotionDex §4.1 / §4.2 / §4.3 (`sampleHz` mis-scales every window on any gap; `respiratoryRate()` is confident across strap-off epochs; no IMU plausibility bound) — recorded so a cleared context cannot lose them, and the reason the parent audit stays PROPOSED. **§5** the fleet-wide backlog: 16 pending changesets awaiting a corpus-verified release, Integrator gap-aware coverage part 2, REM staging §3, fold-by-night-key | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-28)* |
| [`CPAP-SA2-OXIMETRY-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-SA2-OXIMETRY-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — premise REFUTED. There is no second SpO₂ source.** Proposed on the strength of `SA2.edf` existing on **194 nights at a median 6.83 h**, a wired oximeter immune to the ring’s BLE dropouts. Every one of those numbers is accurate, and the conclusion was still wrong: the ResMed writes `SA2.edf` on every therapy night **whether or not the optional oximeter accessory is attached**, filling both channels with the physical value **−1** when it is not. **193 of 194 nights are entirely that fill.** The accessory was attached exactly once — 2026-06-13, 2.50 h — which is the corpus total. §1 measured the presence of a **file** and reported it as the presence of **data**; a full-length, well-formed, perfectly readable 7.2 h session containing no measurement still reads as 7.2 h. Overstatement ≈ **194×**. The irony is exact: §1 singled out `20260613_231433_SA2.edf` as the *unrepresentative* file to beware of, and it is the only representative one. **Nothing is broken** — `CpapDsp.oximetryLane` has always required 50–100 % and returns `reason:'oximeter-not-connected'`, so no sentinel ever entered a computation and no published number is affected; the refutation was already sitting in the codebase under that branch name. Ships `tools/cpap-sa2-agreement.mjs` (`--selftest` planted-truth · `--cpap/--ring`), which measures coverage as **data** and implements the comparison for the day the accessory is plugged in. Its selftest also sharpens §2: Pearson r isn’t *pessimistic* about agreement, it is **uninformative** — r swings **0.99 → 0.01** with the planted bias unchanged (shared physiology vs independent sensor noise), while Bland–Altman returns 1.80 % every time. **Decision: nothing consumes SA2 — not "agreement too poor", but n = 0.** | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **Reference write-up for anyone harvesting a CPAP card over an access-point Wi-Fi SD adapter** — everything `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST` cost to learn, measured on the real ResMed + ez Share card, none of it from a datasheet. **The listing CEILS** (`listed == ceil(bytes/1024)`, 10/10 files) — so the natural symmetric `|received - printed| <= half-a-digit` completeness test rejects **every file whose fractional KB part is below 0.5**, about half of them, forever; that shipped, and produced **487 `.part` files / 246 MB** of byte-perfect downloads rejected as truncated, re-fetched and re-rejected on every run. Judge against `Content-Length` (exact, always present); if you must use the listing make the window **asymmetric** `(P-q, P]`, because a symmetric one opens a band *above* P where a corrupt file passes. Also: the card supports Range/206 and runs at 1.65 MB/s (a night is 2.9-4.7 MB, so skip-if-present — not bandwidth — is what makes this cheap); the download needs **no privilege at all** and every root requirement exists only to join the AP; the supplicant control dir must be writable **by the service** (wpa_supplicant runs as root and does not need to own it) and must **never** be the system's, or a bare `wpa_cli terminate` kills the box's own supplicant; `wpa_cli` **cannot run under `ProtectSystem=strict`** at all (its client socket lives in `/tmp`) — read `/sys/class/net/<if>/carrier` instead, which is the same fact with no socket and no privilege; a non-zero `wpa_supplicant -B` means one is already running, not that association failed; **guard the default route** (the card routes nowhere, and taking it makes a headless box unreachable until someone walks over); do not harvest while recording (5-7 dB and 17 BLE reconnects measured), and schedule it in a bounded window, not on an hour floor. **Station mode removes the entire privileged branch** and is strictly better where the deployment allows it. Closes with the `.part` conventions that mattered and a debugging order — the failures all present as "it pulled nothing" and the log line names the wrong cause more often than the right one | Reference *(living · last-verified 2026-07-28)* |
| [`ANDROID-CAPTURE-FORMAT-CONTRACT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ANDROID-CAPTURE-FORMAT-CONTRACT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **The interface between Tepna and a separate Android capture app — the contract is the FILES, not an API** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Companion to `ADD-AN-ADAPTER.md`: that covers adding a NEW layout, this covers matching an EXISTING one exactly. Tepna already parses **Polar Sensor Logger** output — the committed corpus *is* PSL files and `dex-ingest.js:35` matches its naming by regex — so a phone needs no Tepna code and Tepna needs no app code; it needs PSL-named files with zone-free civil stamps plus the ViHealth CSV for the ring. **Licence boundary recorded:** PSL itself is CLOSED SOURCE (Google Play, Jukka Happonen / Polar Research Centre — there is nothing to fork, and nothing needs forking); `polar-ble-sdk` is **NOASSERTION** (Polar's own terms, read before depending); `lrq3000/open-polar-h10-ecg-logger` is **GPL-3.0 and stale since 2020** — *do not read it*, same clean-room call as `engzee_ecg_detector`. Our own `oxyii.py` crosses freely (Apache-2.0) and carries the expensive knowledge: O2Ring-S is a **T8520/OxyII device, NOT legacy Viatom**, `0xA5` framing, CRC-8 poly 0x07, XOR auth, **no bond, no AES**. §3 is the clause that silently breaks everything — **zone-free LOCAL CIVIL time, never epoch ms, never a zoned instant**: a phone writing epoch produces files that parse, load, render a plausible night and are wrong by the viewer's UTC offset, with no warning. Also pins the `timestamp [ms]` **relative+fractional** rule (integer+absolute shipped once and made ECGDex infer fs=143 not 130 — a silent ~10% HR error). §5 is the acceptance test: a phone file and a box file for the same signal must be interchangeable, including a **different-timezone render** — the only cheap test for §3. §6 records that the recorder shape IS achievable (PSL proves it via a foreground service); the untried part is a second, independent BLE stack for the ring alongside the Polar SDK in one app | Brief *(REFERENCE living 2026-07-26)* |
| [`VIGIL-PPG-GRID-AUDIT-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-PPG-GRID-AUDIT-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md) | **An `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` pass over `capture-host/` — the O2Ring PPG grid was inventing elapsed time, plus two guards failing OPEN** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). **§1 the finding:** the 2026-07-22 honest-gap fix (`O2RING-PPG-GAP §1`) measured gaps **frame-to-frame** and could only ever advance, so it **rectified symmetric BLE arrival jitter into monotonic fabricated time** — **+210 s of elapsed time that never happened over 11.18 h of real corpus**, while `rows/wall` sat at nominal on *every* file (nothing was actually lost); the daemon logged it as "% of the session's real time was lost by the link". Synthetic replay at this code's own documented jitter (sd 16.4 ms) inflates a **zero-loss** stream **+3.2 %** with **1264 phantom gaps**. Downstream: PpgDex's `fs` survives (it takes the *median* ns delta) but `relSec` — the **beat timeline** — is stretched, ~23 % of inter-beat intervals inflated by a median 48 ms on a ~1230 ms RR, hitting RMSSD (a successive-difference metric) on the **finger** leg validated against chest ECG. **Fix:** anchor the grid to the **session start**, not the previous frame, so jitter cancels instead of accumulating — replayed old-vs-new on the same corpus, **+1.05 % → +0.05 %** inflation, hundreds of gaps/file → single digits; the residual slow correction toward the NTP-disciplined host clock is deliberate and now pinned by an **outcome**-based test. **§2** `TelemetryBus.push` silently rewrote a stream's declared channel count (`m.chans = nch`) — now kept, breach surfaced via `shape_errors()`/`shapeError` and the malformed frame dropped from the live ring; logged not raised (a raise escapes into bleak's D-Bus dispatch and is swallowed — quieter, not louder). **§3** an unreadable NTP `Stratum` (`16.0`, `n/a`) parsed by `.isdigit()` → `None` → graded **`disciplined`/`absolute_ok:true`** — a fail-open on the one field gating absolute-time trust; now `_num()` + `stratum_unparsed` → holdover, while a genuinely absent stratum keeps its documented benefit of the doubt. **§4** `parse_rssi` accepted up to **+20 dBm**, so BlueZ's no-measurement sentinel landed in the LINK sidecar as a reading (measured `0`/`+1`/`+8` on 2026-07-25); bound to `-1`, and the existing test that asserted `0 == 0` changed deliberately. **§6 records nine hypotheses that did NOT survive execution** (ECG timing columns agree to 0.005 %; Verity grids clean; PpgDex `fs` refuted; `_HR`/`_RR` split clean; no snapshot race — neither path contains an `await`; `telemetry.py` is structurally exempt from scientific error) so a later audit does not re-spend the effort. **Gate:** capture-host pytest **947 → 971 green**, 0 regressions; the headline test is **mutation-checked** (FAILs the old algorithm at +3.05 %, passes the new at +0.026 %) after a first draft with bounded jitter passed under both — reproducing the very blind spot that let the defect ship. **§5 residue CLOSED:** re-derivation of the already-captured nights was investigated and is **impossible** — frame boundaries are invisible in the jitter-free case (intra-frame and cross-boundary phone steps are both exactly `1/fs`) and the phone column is ms-quantized against a 7.953 ms true step, so any repair would fabricate a grid to replace a fabricated one; new **`capture-host/ppg_grid_check.py`** therefore MARKS instead, separating *fabricated* from genuinely *lossy* by `rows/wall` (corpus: **74 inflated · 17 lossy · 128 ok · 1071 too short to judge**, **+727 s** fabricated). The deferred `pr or 0` fabricated pulse rate is also fixed — measured headlessly against the shipped `oxydex-dsp.js`, `0` and blank are rejected **identically**, so the deferral reason was unfounded and the blank moves no downstream number. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-25)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **27 correctness defects across `capture-host/` — `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` executed against the host layer** (out-of-suite: no bundle, no `manifestHash`, no fixture). 7 parallel dimension hunters, each faced by an adversarial verifier instructed to KILL its findings; 30 candidates → 27 confirmed, 3 refuted, and **7 ship with a verifier correction that changed the mechanism or the blast radius**. The three worst share one shape: *state that models "the session" but is scoped to something else.* **§A1** the absorbed DST shift is scoped to the PROCESS under a `Restart=always` unit with no `RuntimeMaxSec`, so one autumn fall-back stamps every later night an hour off — in the Phone column, the FILENAME and `night_dir()` — and a deliberate `timedatectl set-timezone` is swallowed identically while `/api/clock/tz` answers ok. **§A2** a >1 h outage makes `nightqc` discard the pre-outage half and grade the remainder `coverage: 1.0, ok: true` (the measured 2026-07-24 silence came within 85 s of firing). **§A4** the "% captured" badge had THREE independent denominator defects and was wrong in both directions on real corpus — 16.7 % on a flawless night, 196.7 % on a real H10 ACC. Plus: one failed webhook silenced an offline alert for the whole episode with no journal line either way (**§C1/§C2**); `.archived` was a one-way latch on a night that can still grow, and the retention gate accepted a half-made mirror — **the only finding that deletes data** (**§C4**); a 0–2 % truncated CPAP EDF was accepted, reported ok and skipped forever (**§C5**); the watchdog still read `connected` as `streaming`, the confusion `1f6bcdf` fixed one module over the same day (**§C3**); an unparseable POST body deleted the offload target and answered 200 (**§D1**); and the test suite reloaded the developer's real systemd, costing ~50 s of polkit timeouts per run (**§E6**). **ALL 27 EXECUTED 2026-07-26** — every fix with a test verified RED against the pre-fix code, every over-correction risk with a control green in both directions, and **six assertions that PINNED a defect green deliberately inverted** (`test_settings_save_failure_is_swallowed` was the defect written down). Three findings changed under execution: §A3's obvious fix is a **runaway** (+1.04 % inflation, 118 phantom gaps on a ZERO-LOSS stream), its test file **re-implemented the code it tested** — which is why the defect survived a prior audit — and §C7's owned bundles were never broken (the audit counted `data-inline-src="…"`, which ends in `src="`). Gate: capture-host pytest **1391 → 1471 green**. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-26 — all 27 shipped)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | Residue from executing the capture-host deep audit. **§1.1** the §A3 sample-rate estimator cannot measure before 30 s has elapsed, so a flat **+0.227 s per session** is frozen into the ns column — a one-off, not a rate (measured unchanged from 1 min to 10 h), correctable only by persisting the per-device rate across sessions, which is the cross-session state §A1 was about and needs its own decision. **§1.2** `Science.html` links to 15 analysis harnesses that must NOT be served, and `index.html` references a `feed.xml`/`sitemap.xml` the repo does not contain — invisible to `sync-apps.sh`, which can only copy what exists; a link-checker over the SERVED tree is the honest gate. **§2** records what was deliberately not taken and why: `ruamel.yaml` for comment-preserving config writes (a SOUP entry for a cosmetic gain on a 62304-aligned appliance) and §D1's target-less disable path (the brief's Fix text and its own verifier correction disagree; the correction won, and reversing it is a UI-contract change needing the owner). **§3** carries the parent's §G scope forward verbatim so no unexamined area reads as verified — ~15 swallowed `except` sites, the **backward NTP step that genuinely rewinds the Phone column** (the mirror of §A1, now cheap to fix since `open_sample_writers()` exists), no hardware exercise, the frame decoders never differentially diffed, `pull_session.py`'s partial-download path (the same defect §C5 fixed, one module over), and the live box never inspected — including its unresolved `cpap.state: "error"`. **§4** the process lesson: **a test that re-implements its subject tests nothing**, which appeared three times in one execution and is the test-side twin of the charter's *sibling divergence* class. | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-26)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-27-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-27-BRIEF.md) | **Residue from executing DEEP-AUDIT-III — and the process facts that outlived the fixes.** 24 of its 28 confirmed findings landed across 16 merged PRs (#449–#464), 3903 → **4056 assertions**; five items remain and are specified here: GlucoDex's `coverage` + `timeseries` (the sibling of §6.2, which also starves the Integrator's windowed autonomic-glycemic path); the **surge-side twin of the desat double-count** — found by mutation-checking §3.1's own fix — where two cardiac observers double `lambda` and push the Poisson null toward `belowChance`, **suppressing real findings** rather than inflating a count; `ansBalance()`'s fabricated `hf || 1` denominator, which needs a DECISION (what does SNS/PSNS read when HF is zero?) not a patch; OxyDex's `_durBad`, still one-sided after §1.2 removed the cause; and `tools/regen-integrator-goldens.mjs`, the one empty cell in the coverage matrix. **§2 is the part worth keeping:** **six fix sketches were wrong and only execution showed it** (a `dtMs` merge that would have UNDER-counted apneas, a p<0.05 verdict that was arithmetically unreachable on 10 surrogates, a 1 s clock slack that still rolled a day at 2 s, a `durSec` that would have fabricated 29 continuous days, a strict overlap rule that would have dropped whole nodes from fusion, and a headline whose real damage was the match-rate KPI rather than the index) — so a brief's fix sketch is a lead to re-derive, not an instruction, and two of the six would have shipped a NEW defect. Plus: a test that passed for the wrong reason and a group that early-returned before exercising the defect (mutation-check the BEHAVIOURAL assertion); a gate that was blind rather than green (widening the Clock lint 70 → 108 files immediately exposed a SECOND blind gate that could not see two render surfaces at all — they were not classified, they were invisible); the doc outranking the registry when the registry entry is the newcomer (`cohesion-badges` caught three grades being upgraded on a new author's say-so); `tools/build.mjs` not being the whole re-bundle (missed twice — docs, then 8 analysis pages with the DSP in worker blobs); an unpinned `npx biome` reading clean while CI failed on the same file; and a lane that cannot know an answer having to SKIP rather than fail. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-28 — its OWN §1/§2/§3 scope: §1.1–§1.5 all shipped, §1.3 decided `null` by the owner, §2.5/§2.6/§3 folded into `CONTRIBUTING.md`. The parent `DEEP-AUDIT-III` deliberately stays PROPOSED — four of its sections carry no fix stamp and were never in this brief's scope; they are carried in `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS` §4 rather than stamped away)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md) | **Closing the punch list found two fixes guarding nothing.** `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS` §4 carried four `DEEP-AUDIT-III` items as unowned on the reasonable assumption that no fix stamp meant no fix; **all four were already fixed** (2026-07-27, parent never re-stamped). What was not already true is that they were **gated** — established by mutation, the only evidence this suite accepts. **§3.6 had no gate at all**: the one test naming it asserts GlucoDex carries a cell trace (`FOLLOWUPS §F1.1`) — true, and *upstream* of the defect, so nothing pinned the arithmetic and a revert would re-ship a coupling computed from the ECG side alone. **§4.1's gate was pointed one function away**: it drives `respiratoryRate`, which **does not call `sampleHz`** (the defect's home; its only consumers are `actigraphy:418` and `respiratoryEffort:974`). Measured cost of that blindness — reverting `sampleHz` to count÷span drops the derived native rate **26.00 → 20.80 Hz** on a 20 %-holed stream and moves the **published `amplitudeG` +25 %**, with the whole suite green. Both closed with groups asserting the property AT its own seam, verified RED; §4.3's gate was already sound (4 red under mutation); **§4.2 remains PARTLY gated** — its reporting half (covered/brpm-null/denominator) is pinned, its *tracking* half is not: `respViterbi` is a GLOBAL ridge track, so replacing the uniform likelihood with the interpolated line's spectrum steers clean windows too, and reds nothing. Needs a fixture whose true rate CHANGES across the hole. §3 declines a MotionDex brief (it would be an empty container) and declines re-auditing the other 24 FIXED sections here — proposing it as its own work-unit: **how many more fixes are guarding nothing?** | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`QC-SCOPE-RESOLUTION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/QC-SCOPE-RESOLUTION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **QC judged the wrong folder on every night that crossed midnight.** On 2026-07-28 the box reported **nine missing streams** every ten minutes from 00:10 to 04:20 while recording **942 MB of continuous tri-device sleep data** — 6 h 13 m of ECG, arm PPG, finger pleth and SpO₂.
