A device/config fault, **not** a Tepna bug; the Tepna gap is the blindness (`integrator-dsp.js:3320`, `toleranceSec` default **120 s**, against a node **2,370 s** away). **Found sideways** while asking `MULTINIGHT-CORPUS-FINDINGS-FOLLOWUPS` §1.1 whether OxyDex PB episodes land inside device CSR spans (0/20 — but chance was ~1.1, so that proved nothing); what stood out was the first PB episode landing at **exactly +24 min** after the CSR span on two separate nights. Tested against a pairing with a KNOWN lag — apnea→desaturation is 10–40 **seconds** by circulation time — cross-correlating CPAP `_EVE.edf` TAL times (no DSP in the path) against OxyDex desat events over ±60 min: **peak 6.21× over floor at +39.5 min, 240 coincidences vs 27 at lag 0, which is BELOW the random floor of 38.7** (36 nights, 807 CPAP events, 536 desats). **Not a parse bug** — every EDF header start time was checked against the ResMed filename, which encodes it independently: they agree on every file. **Not drift, not a pooling artifact** — estimated PER NIGHT, **27 of 32 nights land within ±5 min of 39.5** with their own 4.7–23.5× peak-to-floor, stable across seven weeks. **The CPAP is the outlier, not the oximeter** — repeating against a second independently host-captured node (ECGDex `autonomic_surge`) gives **4.28× at +38.0 min**, again nothing at zero; the three host-captured signals agree with each other by construction, since `trio-batch` requires a three-way overlap to accept a night and accepts 37. 39 min is no timezone and does not grow over seven weeks: a clock set approximately once. **Breaks:** every CPAP co-observation (`alsoObservedBy`, the apnea-confirmation path, the redundancy accounting `INTEGRATOR-FUSION-ISSUES` §3.1 protects) has run on an empty intersection; `tools/cpap-oxy-couple.mjs`; and §1.1 itself is **void as measured** — it was measuring the clock, not the detectors. Per-night aggregates are unaffected, so §1.2's AHI-vs-hypoxic-burden decorrelation stands. **Proposes:** estimate the lag that maximises co-occurrence and REPORT a suspected skew rather than a quiet nothing (a fusion that finds zero overlap between two busy nodes has learned something and currently discards it) — and explicitly **do NOT auto-correct**, which would make the fusion look right while resting on an estimate and mask the real fix. Same fail-closed discipline as `DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS` §C2  **§2b ROOT CAUSE, part-identified 2026-07-29:** the AirSense exposes no time-of-day setting but does expose a TIMEZONE, and it was on **GMT−5** for a device in **Asheville**, which is GMT−4 in late July — so the machine ran exactly **60 min behind** local civil time. That does not account for the measured **39 min**: 60 − 39 leaves the machine's own clock running **~21 min FAST**, with no exposed control to fix it. **Falsifiable prediction:** correcting the timezone adds 60 min, so it should NOT zero the offset but FLIP it — nights after 2026-07-29 should read **≈ +21 min AHEAD**; a reading of ≈0 would instead refute the 21-min inference and expose a measurement bias. **Two design consequences:** the corpus now contains a **STEP CHANGE**, so a pooled per-device offset must detect a step and be versioned by date rather than averaged across the discontinuity ("alert if it drifts" was too weak — this is a reconfiguration, and it is instant); and this is a **Clock Contract interaction**, since the CPAP's EDF carries local civil time with NO zone and Tepna stores it as floating `tMs`, so a timezone change shifts every later timestamp by a whole hour with nothing in the file to say so. The offset is a property of device CONFIGURATION, not only oscillator drift — a device that travels or crosses DST steps again, which is why the anchor estimator's per-anchor offsets are the right shape: they measure *when* as well as *how much*.| Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31 — residual ≈21 min confirmation owned by `POOLED-CLOCK-FIT`)* |
| [`OXYDEX-PB-OVERCALL-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/OXYDEX-PB-OVERCALL-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **OxyDex emits periodic breathing on 92 % of nights; the machine scores it on 13 %.** Over **39 paired nights** OxyDex emits a `periodic_breathing` ganglior event on **36**, the ResMed's device-scored PB on **5** — chance-corrected agreement **κ = −0.039**, not weak agreement but *none* (2×2: both 4 · device-only 1 · OxyDex-only 32 · neither 2; reproduce with `tools/pb-agreement.mjs`). **It is the `estimatedAHI` shape one node over** — a detector firing on nearly every night carries no information whatever its threshold was tuned to, and it reaches the user with an imperative attached (*"CS pattern likely — review CPAP pressure"* on 9 nights in 10) AND the fusion layer as a `ganglior_events` currency, where an always-on channel degrades every observer-counting rule. **Explicitly NOT established:** the device scores flow with its own thresholds and is not ground truth, so disagreement ≠ OxyDex is wrong; n=1 subject; and the threshold's derivation has not been read — it may faithfully implement a published rule whose base rate does not fit a treated-CPAP subject, in which case the honest fix is wording/tier, not a number. Prescribes: read the basis before touching a threshold, measure the operating point rather than guess it (tuning to improve κ on 39 nights of one subject would be overfitting, and "no defensible threshold" is an allowed outcome), temper the imperative regardless, and check the fusion blast radius. **§4 items 1-2 ANSWERED 2026-08-01 — the threshold has no citation, and the sweep shows it is not measuring periodicity.** The gate is `lowMotion && sustained && cross >= 6` against an **absolute** 95 % level; its three constants describe themselves as "detector tuning" and "algorithmic", with no paper and no clinical criterion — and the gate contains **no periodicity test at all** (`cycleLen` is computed into `meta` after the decision, and never gates anything), where AASM scores CSR on a 40-90 s cycle, >=3 consecutive cycles and a crescendo-decrescendo envelope measured against the patient's OWN baseline. The sweep (`tools/pb-operating-point.mjs`, driving the shipped `processNight`) over 37 nights: flagged **36/37 (97 %)**; overnight mean SpO2 **94.6-96.6 %**, so the baseline straddles the crossing level every night and the trace sits within +/-1 % of it for a median **64 %** of the night — on INTEGER-quantised 1 Hz data, where 94/95/96 dither crosses `>= 95` continually with no periodicity. Episode count tracks **% of night below 95 % at r = 0.893** and mean SpO2 at r = -0.821: it is measuring mild hypoxemia burden, not periodicity. **So the shape is wrong, not the number** — raising `OSC_FLAG_CROSSINGS` only yields a stricter hypoxemia threshold still labelled "periodic breathing". Third instance of one pattern after `estimatedAHI` and effort-based apnea typing; twice already the answer was to withdraw the claim rather than retune it. **§4 item 4 ANSWERED 2026-08-01 (§6) — the fusion blast radius is real, not inert.** `tools/pb-fusion-blast.mjs` (driving the shipped `adaptEnvelopeNode` + `fusePeriodicBreathing`, no reimplementation) over the **24 nights** where a trio OxyDex export pairs with a CPAPDex one: OxyDex fires on **23/24 (96 %)**, `fusePeriodicBreathing` corroborates on **3/24**, and **0/24** still corroborate with the OxyDex leg removed. **The measurement had to be a counterfactual** — "3 of 24" reads conservative and answers nothing; the informative quantity is how many survive deleting the always-on observer and changing nothing else, and none do. `corroborated` is `nObservers >= 2`, so a leg on 96 % of nights makes it arithmetically a **one-observer rule wearing a two-observer label**, where the deciding observer is the CPAP's own PB scoring — the rater κ = −0.039 was measured against. The KPI publishes `nObservers` as its headline value under *"signals corroborate"*, and the leg joins the noisy-OR at 0.5 × 0.6 = 0.30, lifting a device-only **0.80 → 0.86** on every block it touches. **Bounded:** `fuseApneaEvents` pools by impulse over `desat_event`/`spo2_desaturation` only, so PB reaches neither the confirmed-apnea rule nor `confirmedAHI` — §3.4's analogy to the double-counted apnea index does NOT extend. **One leg was not measured:** the ECGDex cardiac-CVHR observer is **unexercised, not inert** — it reads `apnea.cvhrIndex` and 0 of 24 committed trio ECGDex exports carry an `apnea` block, but that block landed 2026-07-23 (`11091ef`) *after* the corpus was generated (2026-07-12); corpus staleness, and `tools/trio-batch.mjs` would bring it into scope. **No behaviour changed** — every remedy (withdraw the leg · stop calling it corroboration · redesign the detector) is the same owner surface decision as §5.4, and withdrawing the leg silences the fused finding entirely on this corpus with no measured compensating gain. What landed is the measurement plus a **characterization** gate (metamorphic pair holding the OxyDex leg identical while toggling only CPAPDex, plus the 0.80 → 0.86 uplift as a number), labelled as characterization in source so it is not read as endorsement; mutation-verified both directions. Remaining item 3 (the user-facing string) stays with the owner | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01)* |
| [`VIGIL-HARDENING-II-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-HARDENING-II-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md) | **Hardening pass II over `capture-host/` — the DESTRUCTIVE paths** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Pass I took the signal path; this takes the code that can delete a night or destroy the config, with the box at 13.2 % free and retention about to engage. **§1 retention could delete the only copy** — `plan_prune` deleted by AGE alone, so with the backup volume absent the 15th night would have removed a recording whose only other copy was on a disk the box cannot see (measured: age-only would delete `2026-07-16`/`-17` within 6 nights; gated, it deletes nothing). Closes `VIGIL-OVERNIGHT-FINDINGS-2026-07-24` §P3.2. The stall is deliberate and made loud three ways (`status.retention_held`, an edge-triggered warning, and a rewritten low-disk alert — the old text read as "raise keep_nights", the one action that would not help). **§1.2** `active_nights` failed OPEN toward deletion: `except OSError: continue` made an unreadable night (EACCES/EIO/EMFILE) look settled and therefore prunable — the doubt licensed the delete; now only `FileNotFoundError` is inactive, and the test that pinned the fail-open was changed deliberately. **§1.3 the `.archived` marker is not proof the copy survives** — found while testing §1.1 and it would have defeated the gate: the marker records that a copy was MADE, and 6 of 10 nights carried one against a volume that is no longer present, so `unarchived_nights` now requires the marker AND `dest/<night>` to exist, with an absent dest protecting everything. **§2 `config.yaml` was written non-atomically** — `open(path,"w")` truncates first, so a full disk or power cut left the appliance's whole config empty, SILENTLY (it is read once at startup, so it surfaces as "recorded nothing all night" at the next restart); replaced with mkstemp + fsync + `os.replace` + directory fsync, and the read side now refuses a non-mapping config with a message that names the problem instead of an `AttributeError` several frames later. **§3 records five hypotheses that did NOT survive** — IMU units verified on real data (|acc| 998/1028 mg vs 1 g; gyro 3.8 dps at rest); the magnetometer "scale error" is a **false positive** from hard-iron offset, and a sphere fit (bias moves the centre, not the radius) gives **0.4483 G vs Asheville's ~0.48 G**, re-confirming DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22's refutation of the 8× claim — **use the sphere fit, not `|B|`**; the O2Ring `[7]`=PI / `[11]`=motion mapping is correct once off-finger frames are excluded (PI non-zero 100 %, motion 99.8 % zero); `archive_night` cannot mirror onto the boot disk (the caller guards it). **Gate:** capture-host pytest **971 → 984 green**, Node lane 3899 green, `build --check` clean; §2's headline test **mutation-checked** against a reverted `_save` after a first draft reached for the closure, got `None`, and proved nothing. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-25)* |
| [`VIGIL-HARDENING-III-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-HARDENING-III-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md) | **Hardening pass III — the DECODE and CONTROL surfaces** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Closes the four modules passes I/II named unaudited: `polar_pmd` framing internals, `bonding`, `oxyii` decode below the live-frame mapping, and the remaining `webmon` endpoints. **§1 a part-decoded PMD delta frame was stamped as the frame's TAIL** — `_decode_delta` has five guards that abandon a corrupt frame part-way (all added because real corruption was seen: 0.41 % of MAG rows, 2026-07-18), while `decode_frame` back-times from `last_ns`, the stamp of the frame's LAST sample — which a truncated frame never decoded. Reproduced on a synthetic 10-sample ACC frame truncated to 5: every survivor placed **96.2 ms late** at 52 Hz, scaling with how much was lost, and the last survivor claiming a timestamp that belongs to a sample that does not exist. Right values, fabricated times. The true sample count is unrecoverable once a block header is corrupt, so the frame is now DROPPED (`_decode_delta_ex` reports truncation; `decode_frame` raises and `on_pmd` records it in `last_error`) — an honest gap per the Clock Contract. `_decode_delta` stays as a bare-list wrapper so every known-answer test on real Verity frames is untouched; **two tests changed deliberately** — they asserted `len(s)==1` ("only the reference sample survived"), pinning the VALUE protection while blessing the TIME fabrication in its worst form (a lone reference sample is the frame's FIRST, stamped at its END = a whole frame's span of error). **§2 `_valid_mac` accepted a trailing newline** — `.match()` on `^…$`, and Python's `$` also matches BEFORE a trailing newline. Not a command injection (only a lone newline gets through, so nothing can follow it into the `bluetoothctl` script), but `/api/remember` persists the address and one carrying a newline never matches a real BLE address again — precisely the `writers.IDENTITY_FIELDS` failure: "remembered ✓", then silently never captured for the life of the box. Now `.fullmatch()`. **§3 records six surfaces verified clean** — `oxyii` reassembly already resyncs on an implausible length header and bounds its buffer, `decode()` checks magic/complement/length/CRC-8; `polar_pull`'s user-supplied `session` cannot traverse (`strip('/').replace('/','_')` leaves no separator); the device endpoints are allowlisted against `cfg["devices"]`; `bonding` uses `create_subprocess_exec` (no shell); `axis_scale` and `session_restarted` confirmed. **Gate:** capture-host pytest **984 → 992 green**, Node lane 3899 green, `build --check` clean. **§5:** three passes have now found three instances of the same class — fabricated TIME attached to real samples — each invisible because the tests asserted on values and counts, never on placement. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-25)* |
| [`STORAGE-OFFLOAD-TARGETS-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/STORAGE-OFFLOAD-TARGETS-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md) | **A Storage card in the Vigil sidebar — where a finished night goes, and when** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). The box writes ~350 MB/night onto a small SSD, so nights must leave. Seven protocols are offered but **not as equals**, and the card says so: **iSCSI · NFS · NVMe-oF · SMB** are KERNEL MOUNTS needing root, and `capture.py` runs with **`CapEff: 0`** (measured — its own recovery ladder's `hciconfig reset` returned exit 1 for this reason), so for those Vigil stores the target and **generates the exact systemd `.mount` unit + root commands** to install once, then verifies it. **rsync-over-SSH** is implemented end-to-end because the daemon can actually do it; WebDAV/FTP are declared but fail LOUDLY ("not implemented — use a mount or rsync") rather than appearing to work. **The check the feature rests on: `ismount`, not `isdir`** — an unmounted mountpoint is a present, empty, WRITABLE directory on the boot disk, so `isdir` says yes and 350 MB/night lands on the wrong filesystem while the operator believes it is on the NAS; `local` is the deliberate exception. This feeds VIGIL-HARDENING-II §1's retention gate directly — an unready target means nothing is confirmed off-box, so nights are HELD rather than pruned. **No password field exists**: `validate()` rejects `password`/`secret`/`token`/… outright (config.yaml is world-readable and the monitor is LAN-reachable through Caddy), so auth is by reference — an SSH key path for rsync, a root-owned credentials file path for SMB. **These values become argv**, so a leading dash is rejected (`-e/bin/sh` in the host slot would be read by rsync as an OPTION), `--` terminates the operands, there is no shell, and mount `options` are charset-restricted because they land in a generated unit where a newline would append `[Service]` directives. **Schedule** is `after_settle` or `daily at HH:MM` in **local civil time** (Clock Contract — no timezone field on purpose) and `due()` anchors on the WINDOW OPENING, not "≥24 h since last run", which would drift later every day. **Confirmation, not optimism:** a night is marked `.archived` only when a follow-up `--dry-run` finds nothing left to transfer. **Gate:** capture-host pytest **992 → 1035 green**, Node lane 3899 green, `build --check` clean, `monitor.html` JS syntax-checked. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-25)* |
| [`VIGIL-COEXISTENCE-AND-RANGE-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-COEXISTENCE-AND-RANGE-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **Radio coexistence, range recovery, and a journal that hides its own warnings** (out-of-suite; `capture-host/` only, no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). All three items were MEASURED on the box on 2026-07-26 — a CPAP bulk transfer and a deliberate walk-out-of-range overlapped an evening of continuous monitoring. **§1 `capture.py`'s WARNINGs are filed in the journal at PRIORITY=6 (INFO)** — `logging.basicConfig` puts severity in the `%(levelname)s` TEXT only, so `journalctl -p warning` returned nothing across a window holding **33 application warnings** (11 O2Ring + 11 Verity + 10 H10 link errors + 2 STARTUP). Found by making that exact mistake: the overnight watch logged "zero warnings" five times while they sat in the journal. `alerts.py` reads `/api/state`, not the journal, so the product's alerting is UNAFFECTED and this must not be written up as "alerts are broken" — what breaks is every standard operator tool (`-p warning`, journald alert rules, log-shipping severity filters), each returning clean. Fix is a `<N>` syslog prefix (systemd's `SyslogLevelPrefix=yes` is the default, so no unit change and no new SOUP). **§2 WiFi bulk transfer vs BLE, quantified** — 0 reconnects in the 18 min before a CPAP harvest, **16 in the 15 min during it** (Verity 9, H10 7, O2Ring 0); load average 0.34, so NOT starvation. Carries a **methodological correction**: a parallel session measured the same interaction by RSSI over 3 min and inverted the attribution — it reported the Verity unaffected when the Verity is the worst churner (9 reconnects, flat RSSI) and the O2Ring worst when it is the only untouched device (0 reconnects, RSSI *improved* to −57). **RSSI cannot see a link that dropped**; use `link_epoch` deltas over the whole transfer. Extrapolates to ~70 reconnects over a full 65–70 min pull, which is the empirical case for the idle-window rule. **§3 out-of-range recovery PASSES, re-acquisition does not** — all three sensors dropped within ONE 5 s sample and all three self-recovered with no intervention (down 74–90 % of a 16 min window, deepest −100 dBm); the 12.7 min hole was recorded as a NEW SESSION FILE with nothing interpolated, which is exactly what `trio-batch`'s session merge stitches. The weak part is AFTER return to range: the O2Ring re-dropped 4× in 3.5 min, giving the reconnect-backoff work its first measured target (≤1 re-drop per device in the 5 min after recovery). **§4** the CAP_NET_ADMIN ladder is disarmed by design (P1.2) and the one warning saying so is itself unfilterable per §1. **§5** `/etc/udev/rules.d/99-tepna-btdongle.rules` on the box is two fixes behind and needs a password sudo. **§6 records three hypotheses that did NOT survive**, including the CPU-starvation theory that load 0.34 refutes — believing it would have produced a scheduling fix for a radio problem. | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`POSTURE-SIGN-AND-NADIR-LABELS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/POSTURE-SIGN-AND-NADIR-LABELS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Two labels that lied — both found by reading a real vigil night's export against the raw signal, both with GREEN gates throughout** (executes E1+E2 of `VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS`). **(1) MotionDex posture sign INVERTED.** `classifyGravity` mapped `gz < 0 → supine`, but an accelerometer at rest reads **+1 g on the UP axis**, so a chest sensor reads `gz > 0` when supine. The 2026-07-19 night exported `dwellFrac.prone = 1.0` over 3.8 h with zero position changes, while **ECGDex read the identical H10 ACC as `supine`**; measured chest Z was **+973 mg**; owner confirmed supine. Not a judgement call — the fleet already gate-locked the opposite: `ecgdex-dsp.js` uses `uz > 0 ? 'Supine' : 'Prone'` with a test asserting *"+z gravity → supine"*, and PPGDex's posture test feeds `z=+990` as supine. **MotionDex was the sole outlier of three nodes** on a value documented as the *canonical cross-node position vocabulary*. Every gate stayed green because `genSyntheticACC` emitted gravity on **−Z** under the comment "supine torso" — the twin was built to the same inverted belief as the classifier. Fixed classifier + generator + the device-frame comment; the golden **did not move** (both flips cancel — the fixture always *meant* supine and now *is* supine), and the committed twin was regenerated via `genSyntheticACC({seed: 7})` (seed recovered by matching the existing X/Y noise) so **only the Z column changed**, an exact mirror about ±1000 mg. **(2) OxyDex `nadirBins` binned DEPTH under absolute-level key names.** Keys say `above91/b90_91/b88_89/b85_87/below85` but it branched on `e.depth` (drop in points) against 4/6/9/12 — a proxy that only matches the labels when baseline ≈ 95 %; the true `nadir` was discarded one line earlier in a `.map()`. It reported `b88_89: 4` on a night whose **lowest SpO₂ was 91 %**. Both corpus fixtures show the same systematic overstatement — `OxyDex_2026-06-13_1056` `above91: 0 → 9` and `below85: 1 → 0`; `OxyDex_2026-06-25_0439` `above91: 0 → 7` — i.e. it **invents hypoxemia**, always in the same direction. Now carries `nadir` and bins on it, tiling so each event lands in exactly one bin; an event with no finite floor is never binned. **Gates:** suite **3450 assertions / 220 groups green with ZERO skips** under `DEX_UPLOADS=<corpus>`; the equivalence gate **caught both defects before any fixture was touched**; `build.mjs --check` clean across 11 bundles (MotionDex `→dc240e5619dc`, OxyDex `→60a50d07b575`, both orchestrators rebuilt); fixtures regenerated by tool then `verify-fixtures.mjs` stamped 13 `verifiedUnder` off a green corpus run. **(3) The regen loop could not record an INPUT-ONLY change** (found while landing 1): `runRegen()` skipped `rerecord()` whenever the output was byte-identical, but the ledger triple includes `inputHashes` — so E1 (whose two flips cancel, holding the golden while its input moved) stranded GATE B on `input-drift` with no tool able to fix it and hand-editing forbidden. `rerecord()` now no-ops when the triple is already true and is called unconditionally; verified idempotent. **Lesson:** a twin generated by the code under test is a *consistency* check, not a *correctness* check — both bugs needed a real signal read against physics. Follow-ups: a cross-node posture-convention gate · ECGDex still stamps no `motionIndex` · a physical-plausibility check on 100 %-one-posture nights | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`HRVDEX-ALL-NIGHT-SCOPE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/HRVDEX-ALL-NIGHT-SCOPE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **HRVDex was built for ~5-min Welltory SPOT readings; feeding it ECGDex overnight exports puts a different MEASUREMENT UNIT through machinery that assumes the first** — surfaced by running the real corpus end-to-end (50 raw H10 `_ECG.txt` → ECGDex → **28 nightly rows** → HRVDex, 2026-06-06 → 07-13). **Why:** a spot reading is a POINT SAMPLE of the circadian HRV curve (hence `d_rmssd_circ`'s ±8 %/−5 % factors); an overnight recording is an INTEGRAL across ~8 h of that same curve, so the confound is already averaged out and applying the point-sample factor **introduces** a bias rather than removing one — selected by `getUTCHours()`, which for a long recording reflects when recording BEGAN, not physiology. **Measured:** 27 of 28 nights started 21:00–23:00 and **one at 01:06**, so that night was graded `÷1.08` against its neighbours' `÷0.95` — a **12.7 % split inside one homogeneous set**; and **"Morning only" kept 1 of 28**, silently emptying the screen for exactly the data the bridge imports. **Landed:** (1) **`_spanMin`** seeded from the strongest available evidence (`recording.durationMin` → 5-min `timeseries.epochs` grid → summed `sleep.stageMinutes`) and NEVER guessed — **unknown span ⇒ NOT all-night**, so every pre-existing Welltory row is untouched. (2) **`_hrvIsAllNight`** (`ALL_NIGHT_MIN_MIN = 180`, matching `trio-batch`'s `--min-hours`) + **circadian bypass** (`circAdj = 1.0`), publishing `d_all_night` so consumers stop re-deriving from the hour. (3) **`morningOnly` checkbox → three-way `measScope` select** (All · Morning spot · All-night ≥3 h) because these are different units, not two filters over one pool; legacy checkbox still honoured. (4) **The morning band no longer judges an all-night row** — LF/HF's `0.4–2.5 good` is a MORNING-REST reference and the corpus sits at median ≈2.0 with nights at 2.4–3.0, so it would have flagged nearly every night; an all-night row now shows the value with **no verdict** (`neutral`, "morning band n/a"). No overnight band was invented — there is no citable one and §📚 forbids fabricated authority; **an honest '—' beats a number graded on the wrong scale**. Fixing this exposed a real renderer bug: the KPI colour ternary fell through to `--red`, so an ungraded value would have painted as the WORST reading. **Verification:** suite **3462 assertions / 221 groups green with ZERO skips** under `DEX_UPLOADS`; **12 new assertions** lock the regression (identical physiology at 22:00 vs 01:00 starts must agree · a 5-min spot still gets ÷0.95 · unknown span never upgrades · `stageMinutes` fallback); **backward compatibility PROVEN not asserted** — `verify-fixtures.mjs` re-ran the real Welltory fixtures and their **outputs did not move**, only `verifiedUnder` re-stamped; on the real 28 nights rows adjusted went **28/28 → 0/28**. Follow-ups: a properly-cited all-night band · duration-normalised SDNN · high overnight SDNN can be sleep-disordered breathing, not fitness | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`PMD-DECODE-SCALE-AND-RATE-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PMD-DECODE-SCALE-AND-RATE-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Two `polar_pmd.py` defects, found by validating the GYRO/MAG decoders `CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-II` left open** — both produce plausible numbers, no exception, no gate red. (A) **GYRO/MAG are written as raw int16 LSB but labelled `[dps]`/`[G]`** — `decode_frame` (`:271–275`) applies no scale though the device reports `range=[2000]`/`[50]` and `parse_settings_response` already parses it; proof is that written gyro values (max 6000) **exceed the device's own ±2000 configuration**. Fix = `range/2^15`, giving a textbook ±3 dps rest bias. ⚠️ The MAG "field is 2× too strong" reading is a **trap** — a sphere fit (n=81 693) shows it is **hard-iron offset** (centre −494/−188/+1129 LSB, true radius 270.3 LSB → **0.41 G**, correct); MAG takes the same one-line fix. **ACC is native mg and must NOT be scaled** (gravity check: H10 median **1000.9 mg**). (B) **Back-timing assumes the nominal rate** (`:251` `step_ns = int(1e9/fs)`) but the Verity's dies free-run: **MAG 20.516 Hz vs 20** (+2.58%), GYRO 51.684, ACC 51.672, PPG 55.132; H10 ECG is exactly 130.0000. Sign decides the symptom — MAG over-reaches → **678 backwards timestamps** (up to −112.6 ms); GYRO/ACC under-reach → **silent gaps** (22 ms), which is why they looked clean. Fix = derive the step from consecutive `last_ns` + first-frame/dropped-frame guards. **§3.1 kills two hypotheses** (padding over-read — excess is *proportional* to frame size, not constant, so sample counts are correct; `int()` truncation — 6 orders too small). Structural ⇒ affects **every Verity capture including the tri-device σ nights**, but correctable in place (raw LSB is right, `last_ns` is preserved) — no re-recording. §6 records the adjacent O2Ring 359× reconnect churn (link margin, −83 dBm, not code). **§2+§3 LANDED 2026-07-19** (`axis_scale` + device-clock back-timing; 136 pytest pass, each new assertion verified against the pre-fix decoder; real-frame replay: MAG backwards stamps **1042→1**, within-frame spacing now matches end-to-end rate). Execution surfaced two unplanned defects, both gated: **float64 cannot hold a Polar ns timestamp** (~8.4e17 > 2^53 — the offset must be subtracted as an integer) and a frame arriving **closer than nominal** needing a non-overlap clamp. Fresh-capture acceptance **PASSED on the restarted box** (MAG backwards stamps **0**, gyro bias −2.7/+2.3/−1.2 dps, ACC still native mg). **§7 adds the MAG hard-iron calibration baseline:** the scale is confirmed FOUR ways (incl. a magnet-saturation clamp at **exactly 50.0000 G**), and a two-room tumble proves the offset is **intrinsic to the band** (centre moved 0.073 G between rooms) while the **bedroom contributed ~2 G ≈ 4× Earth's field** — so a one-off calibration constant cannot work and any MAG consumer needs **per-session** hard-iron estimation. **§4 acceptance RE-CONFIRMED 2026-07-22** on a larger post-fix segment (`20260720210553`, 554 k MAG samples): **0** non-monotonic MAG stamps, phone-fs≈sensor-fs to **0.00 %** on all four streams, gyro bias −2.85/+2.13/−1.23 dps, MAG sphere radius **0.446 G** — brief flipped **DONE**. §5 corpus rescale has **no committed/shipped impact** (no Verity GYRO/MAG is committed; MotionDex only counts + normalizes to µT at parse, correct for post-fix files); the local pre-fix rescale is a pure-multiply, in-place, deferred until a physical-units consumer ships. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`O2RING-PROTOCOL-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-PROTOCOL-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **REFERENCE — Wellue O2Ring-S (T8520 "OxyII") BLE protocol + capabilities**, reverse-engineered + hardware-validated. It is **NOT legacy Viatom** (OxyII service `e8fb…`, advertises `S8-AW`, Random-Static MAC → resolve by name). `0xA5` frame envelope + **CRC-8 poly 0x07, no AES** (XOR auth w/ MD5 "lepucloud" salt). **Live** (auth 0xFF→setup 0x10→poll 0x04, 24-B header: SpO₂/HR/motion/battery/contact). **Stored-session download** — the `.dat` the phone app syncs: opcodes **0xF1 list · 0xF2 open+size · 0xF3 read-chunk · 0xF4 close**, needs **MTU≥517**; Format A = `[10B hdr][3B SpO₂,HR,status ×N @1Hz][48B trailer, O₂-score×10 @42]`. **Quirks:** advertises ONLY when worn, phone app grabs the single link (close it), short advertising burst (early-exit scan). **Validated:** pulled a 10 h night (SpO₂ 96.3%/HR 50), stored-vs-live SpO₂ MAE **0.76%** / HR **2.42 bpm**; **ring clock ~+151 s off NTP** → stored-`.dat` time needs correction. Code: `oxyii.py`, `pull_session.py` | Brief *(REFERENCE 2026-07-17)* |
| [`O2RING-LIVE-PPG-WAVEFORM-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-LIVE-PPG-WAVEFORM-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **Capture the O2Ring's live ~100 Hz PPG waveform** — the finger-site pleth we currently discard. Each live `cmd=0x04` reply is a 24-B status header (the 1 Hz SpO₂/HR we save) **plus a ~125 Hz raw PPG body** (confirmed by the OxyII reference *and* our own reassembler comment); the onboard `.dat` is **1 Hz only** (decoded: 36 000×3 B = 10 h), so the waveform lives **only** in live BLE traffic and is thrown away every second. **Phase 0** measured the frame on-device (`24+2+count` layout, gap-free); **Phase 1** decoded the body (`oxyii.parse_ppg`, single-channel u8, validated vs the paired ECG @49 bpm); **Phase 2** captures it to the existing PSL ppg layout (no new parser branch) with host-arrival back-timing. **Phase 3** monitor scope card SHIPPED; the **PPGDex round-trip acceptance** and the honest **single-channel** path it depends on are the two remaining Done-when items, both owned by `PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE`. Rate **calibrated to 125.738 Hz** (not the ~100 Hz assumed at scoping); layout now recorded in `O2RING-PROTOCOL` §3b. ⚠️ Until the single-channel path lands, capture writes the one value **replicated across ppg0/1/2**, so PpgDex reports a fabricated `ledAgreementPct: 100` at `measured` tier (`ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` §1.3). A second finger-site optical waveform beside the wrist Verity, one NTP clock. Out-of-suite (`capture-host/`); extends `O2RING-PROTOCOL`. **DONE 2026-07-20** — both remaining Done-when items met: the PPGDex round-trip PASSED on a real capture (`docs/O2RING-FINGER-ROUNDTRIP-2026-07-20.md`) and the honest single-channel path + `ppg1` one-column capture landed (PR #275/#276); the replication warning is closed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`POLAR-OFFLINE-DOWNLOAD-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/POLAR-OFFLINE-DOWNLOAD-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **Polar onboard offline-recording download (PS-FTP), wired into the Vigil monitor** — the Polar sibling of the O2Ring `.dat` puller; executes `POLAR-SDK-CAPTURE` Track A. Pulls the recording a Polar device stores in its own flash, over BLE, from the monitor's Devices view (**📥 Recordings** → list → Download). **USB is HID-only** (`0da4:0008` "Polar INW4J", `/dev/hidrawN`) — no mass storage, BLE is the only path. Protocol verbatim from the Polar BLE SDK: service `FEEE`, MTU char `FB005C51`, **RFC60** length prefix + **RFC76** air-packets (`bit0 next · bits1-2 MORE 0x06/LAST 0x02 · bits4-7 seq`), `PbPFtpOperation{GET,path}` → `PbPFtpDirectory`/raw file; hand-rolled protobuf, **GET-only**. Code: `polar_psftp.py` + `webmon.py` (`/api/recordings`,`/api/pull`) + `monitor.html`. **Bonding is mandatory** (un-bonded read → `UNLIKELY_ERROR 14`). **Hardware finding:** the Verity stores only an **exercise `.BPB` session (HR series, ~55-62 bpm)**, **NO raw-PPG `.REC`** — raw PPG is live-PMD-only; pulled + byte-verified a 7-file session. **Caveat:** trusted-device BlueZ auto-reconnect races the web pull (standalone CLI reliable after `bluetoothctl disconnect`); pause capture + idle device before pulling. Follow-ups: `SAMPLES.BPB`→CSV decoder, web-pull robustness, automate the SDK offline-recording button | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-17)* |
| [`TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **42 hollow gates found by mutation-testing the suite** (executes `audits/TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`). 99 surgical defects planted in the shipped compute code + run against the full suite **with the real corpus** (`DEX_UPLOADS`); **42 stay green under a real defect** (27 high / 15 medium), 55 caught, 2 inconclusive. Each finding = exact `old→new` + why no gate sees it. Highlights: the Bessel **`÷N−1`→`÷N`** SD slip untested in **every** `*-cross.js`; boundary edges never planted (2000 ms beat, worst-10-min window on the last sample, coupling `t+hi`, ambiguous O2Ring night); **surfaced-but-unexported** metrics escape the equiv leg (Baevsky SI **×1000**, PI **×100**, SD1 `/√2`→`/2`); a Supine↔Prone posture flip; 3 provenance-verdict (`manifest-gate.js`) hollows that slip both node lanes. **Scope caveat: JS `run-tests.mjs` lane only — the Python `capture-host/` was NOT mutation-audited** (own pytest CI). Proposes 5 class-killing meta-gates instead of 42 one-offs. **EXECUTED 2026-07-18 — 40/42 closed** (33 both-direction-verified gates, +120 assertions / +7 groups; #59 proven no-op, #98 → follow-up) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |

Fresh mutations in under-covered clusters + a re-scout of the 3 rate-limited-dead clusters → **54 NEW hollow gates**; **48 CLOSED** (all both-direction verified, merged): §CN crossnight 8 (#173), §KR kernel 8 (#175), §IF integrator 8 (#180), §EP ecg-ppg 2/9 (#177), §SI self-ingest 3 (#183), §GV glucodex unexported indices 4 (#184). | [`AUDIT-RECONCILIATION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/AUDIT-RECONCILIATION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Cross-check of the two independent audits run on 2026-07-18** — `DEEP-AUDIT-II` (JS compute paths, 64 defects) vs `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` (the Vigil↔suite seam, 8 findings, 6 of 14 candidates refuted). **Zero contradictions across 72 findings**; where the passes touched the same code they agreed. Three reconciliations applied: (1) the §5 **gyro row credits a defect as a feature** — gyro reaches no positional code path, it only inflates a shared `durSec` denominator, i.e. it *demonstrates* `DEEP-AUDIT-II` §7.3/§7.4; (2) **companion pairing is ONE bug found from both ends** — an unanchored `fnameStampMs` upstream (device serial parsed as the date) plus a guardless `pickNearestByStamp` downstream; fixing either alone leaves wrong-night pairing reachable; (3) **`ledAgreementPct: 100`** — a `measured`-tier consensus statistic over three copies of ONE photodiode, missed by a dedicated badges hunter because the badge and registry agree and only the *producer* reveals the fabrication. Also records the **shared blind spot**: two consecutive passes examined the Integrator's ingest and presentation and skipped its **fusion arithmetic**. Drove `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` to 14 bug classes (+fabricated redundancy · filename-derived semantics · the missing instance · sibling divergence) plus mandatory refuted-claims reporting and a scope declaration. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **16 correctness defects — a third full execution of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`** (a 16-hunter workflow: one hunter per charter bug class + the two chronically under-audited scopes, `capture-host/` and the Integrator's fusion arithmetic). 18 candidates → **16 confirmed / 2 refuted**, each executed in `node:vm` and faced by two adversarial verifiers. Highlights — the Integrator HRV consensus compared PpgDex on the **wander-inflated whole-record SDNN** not `sdnnRobust` (a fabricated ~23 % "divergence"); the **positional-apnea posture leg fabricated a whole night from one snapshot** (the only non-tri-state MotionDex series); three node-local parsers **lacked the §2.7 range guard** (`Date.UTC` silently rolled bad stamps onto wrong instants); ECGDex's orchestrate export **dropped `apnea`/`hrvStability`** so fusion was ingest-route-dependent; an **opening electrode transient collapsed R-peak detection for the whole night**; **34 HRVDex table columns rendered unbadged**. All 16 shipped 2026-07-23 across PRs #377/#378/#380/#381/#383 (+ §D PulseDex MDY, #391 — the one unit missed in the first sweep). **Scope caveat: headless `node:vm` only — the browser lane is not covered.** A node-oriented **pass 2** followed same-day (11 confirmed-new + 1 plausible; shipped via #390/#392/#393). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-23 — all 16 shipped)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-II-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-II-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **64 correctness defects — the second full-fleet execution of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`** (where `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11` found 21). 16 concurrent invariant hunters (the charter's 10 bug classes + 6 end-to-end node traces) established every finding by executing the real modules in `node:vm`; each faced **3 independent adversarial refuters**, then a completeness wave hunted what the method itself missed. Clusters where the first pass and the mutation work did not reach: **node-side fusion, the orchestrators, worker realms, cross-night statistics, and MotionDex**. Highlights — the **Lomb–Scargle Parseval calibration** inflates every ms² band power across 3 nodes on a `validated` metric; OxyDex's severity classifier **saturated on 37/37 nights**; ECGDex **QT/QTc pinned to the window edge** in a committed export with the `unstable` flag inverted; `parseECG` infers fs from a **single ms delta** (130 Hz parsed as 143/167); an 11-site HRVDex **absent-column family** (`null >= 0` is `true`) fabricating DFA α₁~ 1.000 GREEN; the Integrator **silently dropping every event** in multi-record ECGDex/PpgDex/PulseDex exports; cross-night **CV mixes a weighted mean with an unweighted sd**. **Structural finding: six defects survived because a gate asserted the wrong thing, and three of those gates RED on the correct fix** — a second generation of the `TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS` species, plus one feature (coverage-weighting) untested by construction. 43-item punch-list in 4 tiers with sequencing constraints. **Scope caveat: headless `node:vm` only — the browser lane, `capture-host/`, the build tooling and the Integrator's fusion arithmetic are NOT covered.** | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21 — all findings closed+gated; §5 corpus rescale deferred)* |
| [`DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **The residue that needs a HARNESS, not an assertion** (21 gates). §RN the render layer was loaded as raw text but never executed in the node/corpus lane, so no value assertion pinned any surfaced render output — 7 defects shipped green incl. a ~325× mmol glucose error, a green-painted hypoxic SpO₂, "well controlled" on AHI 40. **RESOLVED (path b): a node render-execution harness (PR #187) + hoisted-classifier extractions (PR #191) closed 6 of 7 §RN gates**; 1 pure-canvas finding (ecgdex minute-tick, LOW) deferred. §EP-rest 7 ecg-ppg call-site tolerances (SampEn/PRSA/EDR inside `analyze`) + narrow-band PPG. §AD 7 adapter window-edges (needs an off-suite adapter rig). **§AD-1 CLOSED 2026-07-31 — the ODI-4 × 1.1 surrogate, and it was not what the brief thought.** The "genuinely fixture-heavy" EDF turned out to be a ~50-line in-memory writer the SHIPPED `CpapEdf.readEDF` accepts; the constant named in `nsrr-adapter.js` was **dead code** under a factually wrong comment (`processNight` *does* attach `ahiEst`) and was deleted rather than gated, while the LIVE ×1.1 in `oxydex-dsp computeAHIestimates` had **no test anywhere** and now has one (mutation: `1.1→1.5` flips the surfaced severity band). Writing the test surfaced a **shipped defect**: `NSRR.analyzeRecord` had been dead in production since the bare-global spray was removed (`err:'OxyDex not loaded'` before reading the EDF), and `odi-bias-analysis.js` had the same defect at 4 sites where a `try/catch` read the ReferenceError as "no data" — so that page silently analysed nothing. Both fixed onto `OxyDex._bare`, plus a **class gate** (every bare helper call needs a local namespace binding) since a pin on one site would not have protected the other. Spawned `PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`: with the path restored, `papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` Table 1 no longer reproduces, and its input corpus is gitignored  **ALL THREE CLASSES CLOSED 2026-07-31 — 21/21 gates, every one both-direction mutation-verified.** §RN 7/7 (the last, a canvas minute-tick, needed no shim — the arithmetic was pure and only its LOCATION untestable, so it was hoisted via the same extraction path §RN wave 2 used three times), §EP-rest 9/9, §AD 7/7. Closing them also surfaced two real defects that only appeared because something finally executed the path: `NSRR.analyzeRecord` had been **dead in production** since the bare-global spray was removed, and `crc.respFromEDR` **period-doubles at 24 breaths/min**. Recorded lesson: every one of this brief's five deferrals was blocked on a fixture that did not need building — a recorded deferral is a hypothesis about cost, not a finding | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`ECGDEX-EDR-RESP-ACCURACY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECGDEX-EDR-RESP-ACCURACY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **`crc.respFromEDR` reads exactly HALF at 24 breaths/min.** Spawned by DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS §EP-rest: building the slow-respiration fixture that gate needed produced a rate sweep, and the exported EDR respiration is trustworthy only over roughly **14–22 /min** — biased high below it (8/min reads 11.4, +43 %) and **period-doubled at 24/min** (reads 12). Deterministic across three seeds. Two mechanisms, both at the edges: `_bandResp` is a DIFFERENCE OF MOVING AVERAGES with gentle roll-off, so a fundamental at the 0.4 Hz edge is attenuated enough that the autocorrelation locks onto the second harmonic; and the 4 Hz EDR grid with integer lags quantises the period to 0.25 s. The 2.5–10 s search bounds are **not** the defect and are now both-direction gated. Consequence beyond the number: `f0 = respFromEDR/60` centres `_narrowPhase`, so **CPC/PLV at 24/min is suspect too** — untested, and the more serious half. Recommends parabolic peak interpolation + a harmonic check; guardrail forbids tuning against the synthetic oracle alone **§4 options 1+2 EXECUTED 2026-08-01 — the period-doubling is fixed.** A harmonic check (prefer half the winning lag when admissible and carrying >=0.8x the correlation) plus parabolic interpolation of the autocorrelation peak, both local to `_autocorrPeriod` so no other CRC metric moves. **24/min goes from 12 (-50 %) to 23.4 (-2.5 %)** and the 4 Hz lag quantisation is gone (14/min 15.0 -> 14.7). Sweep MAE halves 1.98 -> 1.02 — but that is almost entirely the one catastrophic point: **excluding 24/min it is unchanged (1.07 -> 1.05)**. **The 8-12/min over-read is UNTOUCHED** (8/min still 11.3, +43 %) — a THIRD mechanism, the fundamental attenuated at the LOW band edge where no harmonic substitutes and no peak survives to interpolate; it needs option 3 (a steeper filter), which moves every CRC metric and was deliberately left. Both halves independently mutation-verified — and the interpolation leg initially PASSED under mutation at +/-0.4 tolerance, so it is now +/-0.15. Records that the ECGDex equiv fixture is the **LIGHT** export and therefore does **not** evidence this change: ECGDex still lacks the rich-export golden PpgDex gained the same day | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01)* |
| [`PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **`papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` Table 1 does not reproduce, and its input corpus is gitignored.** Spawned by DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS §AD-1a. The paper's stated recipe ("open `odi-bias-analysis.html` → Run SubjectA corpus") was **broken for months** — bare `parseCSV`/`processNight` threw inside a `try/catch` that read the failure as "no usable data", so the page populated an EMPTY table silently. Now fixed; with the path restored the five nights give ODI-4 5.6/1.4/1.5/0.5/0.8 against a published after-column of 12.0/14.9/1.9/0.8/0.8 — matching **neither** published column. The apparatus is CONTROLLED (the same realm reproduces the GATE-B-verified `OxyDex_2026-06-13_1056_summary.json` at odi4 1.9 exactly), so the gap is real. The **cause is deliberately not asserted**: `uploads/synthetic/` is gitignored, so the corpus is not a pinned artifact and `genSynthetic` has demonstrably moved (the Mayer-wave correction) — likelier than detector drift given night 2 shifts an order of magnitude, but an inference, not a finding. The real defect is **provenance**: every other known-answer here is content-addressed; the corpus behind a *published* claim is the one that is not. Guardrails forbid tuning the detector to recover the table  **ANSWERED 2026-08-01 — it is the INPUTS, and the mechanism is measured.** The discriminating experiment holds inputs fixed and varies only code: the repo's EARLIEST committed detector (2026-07-01) on today's corpus reproduces today's numbers *exactly*, so detector drift is excluded. The corpus was **never tracked in git at any point** and its ground truths record no seed or generator version. It is internally coherent (severity ranks agree across ahiTarget, planted events, minSpO₂, %<90) — which makes the finding worse, not better: night 2 spends **24.7 % below 90 %** yet scores **ODI-4 1.4/h**, because the detector finds 242 desaturations and correctly **excludes 232 as artifact**. The synthetic desats fall at up to **4 %/s** against `SELFGATE`'s 1.5 %/s physiological ceiling (32 % of falls past it on night 2, 16 % on night 1, ≤4 % on the mild nights). Since the generator plants more events on severe nights, the rejection **scales with severity** — reproducing the paper's headline severity-dependent deficit **with no detector bias present**. Table 1 was deliberately **NOT** "corrected" to the new numbers (they measure the gate rejecting a bad fixture) and the corpus deliberately **NOT** pinned (that would enshrine the defect). Standing tool `tools/synth-desat-kinetics.mjs`; paper carries a status banner; spawned `SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01` | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01)* |
| [`SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — and the ODI-4 paper’s central finding does not survive it.** `synth-gen` glided SpO₂ with a first-order lag at `k = 0.28`, whose *initial* rate is `k × depth`, so a 15 % desaturation opened at **4.2 %/s** against the **1.5 %/s** ceiling `selfGateDesat` uses to separate a systemic desaturation from a probe squeeze. OxyDex rejected them **correctly** — **232 of 242** events on the AHI-38 night, ODI-4 **1.4/h** — and because severe nights plant deeper events the rejection scaled with severity, reproducing the severity-dependent deficit `papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` reports as its central result, with no detector bias present. Saturation is **rate-limited**, not exponentially relaxing, so the fix imposes the limit directly rather than shrinking `k` (which would flatten shallow events that were never the problem). The caps are **measured, not inherited from the brief that asked for them**: 36 real nights of `timeseries.spo2` + 342 events carrying `onsetTMs` → onset→nadir p50 **7 s**, depth p50 **5 %**, implied **0.714 %/s**, only **0.20 %** of real falls over the ceiling. Detector unchanged, ODI-4 vs planted AHI goes slope **0.051 → 0.946** (R² 0.137 → 0.997) and the deficit flattens from 2.2→36.6 to a constant 2.2–4.9 — **the same 242 events are found either way; the difference is entirely the gate.** Pinned as a **re-derived** fixture (the nights render in-realm every run) rather than 3.7 MB of gitignored bytes CI never re-derives; restoring the old glide reds all eight assertions. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`SYNTH-GEN-FIXTURE-REALISM-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/SYNTH-GEN-FIXTURE-REALISM-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **The SpO₂ generator’s defect is not present in the PPG or RR generators — and the RR generator has the opposite one.** Answers the one line `SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS` left open, which was deliberately not asserted in passing. **PPG: no defect** — five synthetic nights through `PPGDSP.parsePPG → analyze` sit *inside* the 36-night real envelope on every quality metric (`correctionRate` 1.5–3.5 vs real median 4.25, `analyzablePct` 98–99 vs 97), nowhere near rejection; now gated against the real p10/p90. **RR: the opposite failure** — **0.00 %** Malik-corrected on all five synthetic nights against a real median of **4.73 %** (range 0.66–15.65). Named separately because they look alike and are not: a **rejected** fixture makes the measurement silently become a measurement *of the gate* and produces confidently wrong numbers (it produced the ODI-4 severity deficit); an **over-clean** fixture produces no wrong number, only unwarranted confidence, because a path that matters on real data is never exercised end-to-end. Deliberately **not fixed**: `correctRR` keeps its own injected-ectopic gate, injecting ectopy would move every HRV fixture for a realism gain nobody asked for, and speculative fixture-tuning is the habit this wave exists to correct. Also discharges the follow-up `IBI-ALIGNMENT-LIMIT` owed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **What an EXECUTED audit of the Vigil↔suite seam found — and the 10 plausible claims it killed.** 14 candidate findings run against the real engines (29-agent verify + adversarial-refute pass); **6 did not survive**. Live: (1) `signal-orchestrate.js:398` `fnameStampMs` is **unanchored**, so the 8-digit H10 serial parses as the date — two nights 3 days apart both → `0292-01-07`, silently disabling `pairCompanions`' nearest-stamp tiebreak (the anchored fix already exists at `dex-ingest.js:45` with the comment explaining why); (2) capture-host's **contiguous** stamp makes `deviceKey` null, so `planIngest` stops setting aside foreign-device sidecars (executed: Verity ACC enters the H10 lane) — fix app-side, the real PSL corpus is underscore-form; (3) the O2Ring pleth written as `(v,v,v)` makes `ledAgreementPct` **fabricate 100%** on a 1-photodiode device across 5 surfaces at **`measured`** tier (HRV byte-identical — reporting defect, not compute); (4) `pat-feasibility` decides its verdict on **uncorrected** drift (`cpCorr` computed, rendered, never re-gated) + an undocumented 60–700 ms `physical` window — **fix before `INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR` Phase 0**; (5) PpgDex `respRate` needs **3** fixes not 1 (correcting `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-FOLLOWUPS §2`). **§5 is a do-not-re-open list**: OxyDex 1 Hz desat drift (0 s error — the 422/849 s figures are a *post-fix regression note*), CPAP ventilation lane (fixed `2c83127`), **`nCorneredHat` does not exist** (N≥4 is a corpus gap, not a coding task), gyro-is-metric-dead (it moves `dwellFrac`), MotionDex-unreachable-from-OverDex (export path fuses it) **§1.1 EXECUTED + gated 2026-07-18:** the open question was answered by running `pairCompanions` over the real 250-file `Ecg nightly/` H10 corpus — **147 of 153** companion slots paired to the WRONG NIGHT (collapse is per-MONTH: 34 June primaries all got the `20260606` sidecars, 17 July all got `20260701`); anchored `fnameStampMs` takes it to **153/153**. Bounded to multi-night drops (OverDex folder-walk / multi-night Unifier); single-night drops and the `dex-ingest.js` app path were always correct. Gated with a **numeric-id** two-night fixture — every prior `pairCompanions` test used a lettered id, which structurally cannot reproduce it. **§1.4 EXECUTED 2026-08-01 — its blocker was STALE.** §1.4 sat blocked on (ii) *"`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE` is PROPOSED — PpgDex has no single-channel and no finger-site path"*; that brief has been **DONE since 2026-07-20**, verified on hardware, and `parsePPG` already returned `site:'finger'` with a single-channel beat lane. Checked in the code, per this brief's own §0. The tie (`oxydex-spo2` 0.95 on the vendor token alone vs `polar-sense-ppg` 0.85 on the `_PPG` suffix, gap 0.10 < the 0.15 threshold) meant the ~125.7 Hz finger pleth routed **ambiguous** and was never analyzed as PPG in either host. Fixed with a new `adapters/o2ring-ppg.js` (0.97, REFERENCES `PpgDex.parsePPG`) plus two symmetric **declines** rather than out-bidding: `oxydex-spo2` declines a `_PPG` waveform stream, and `polar-sense-ppg` declines a *foreign vendor's* `_PPG` — letting it win the tie would have stamped "Polar Verity Sense / OH1" provenance on a Wellue waveform, worse than the ambiguity. Unknown-vendor `_PPG` still takes the 0.85 default. **Fixing (i) exposed a third, latent defect** — the layout→site rule lived only inside `parsePPG`, so `compute()`'s SignalFrame branch rebuilt a rec with **no `site`** and the export fell through to `rec.site || 'wrist'`: invisible while `polar-sense-ppg` was the only ppg-frame source (3-LED, so right for the wrong reason), and a **wrist-validated morphology tier on a fingertip pleth** the moment a finger layout could route. `deriveSiteFromLayout` is single-sourced now. So blocker (ii) was right in substance, wrong in its stated reason: the danger was the adapter boundary discarding `site`, not a missing finger model. Measured end-to-end: a 7.4 h `Wellue_O2Ring-S_…_PPG.txt` routes 0.97 and exports `site:"finger"` + 8 events (previously `ambiguous`; forced through, `site:"wrist"`); Verity unchanged. `computeHash` **moved** `c7a8e6dea17d → 2acf0985e625` — not export-inert, so `verify-fixtures.mjs` was re-run on the real corpus and re-stamped the PpgDex equiv fixture; 13 bundles rebuilt. §1.2 (widen `deviceKey` for the contiguous capture-host stamp) still owed. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01 — §1.1, §1.4 done)* |
