| [`INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Suspected LIVE bug found while wiring §Phase 2:** OxyDex's canonical `ganglior.node-export` envelope reaches the Integrator via the **generic normalizer** (`integrator-dsp.js` @525, `return recs` @907), which extracts only 3 pulse fields — so `adaptOxyDex`'s rich summary (`odi4`, `minSpo2`/`meanSpo2`, `hypoxicBurden`, `desatCount` + synthesized desat/arousal events) is **unreachable** for the current export format (the `try{}` at @976 only runs for the legacy non-schema shape). Reproducible: adapting the committed OxyDex golden yields a summary with **exactly 3 keys** (`pulseHr1Hz, rmssd1Hz, hrVarSd1Hz`) and `nEvents:0` — no ODI/SpO₂/hypoxic-burden/desat reach cross-node fusion, so fusion tests may be green because OxyDex contributes nothing. Fix options: port the rich extraction into the generic branch, or route the envelope to `adaptOxyDex`; add a regression gate asserting the OxyDex summary carries `odi4`/`meanSpo2`/`hypoxicBurden`. §5 also flags the PpgDex **rich** export (what the Integrator actually reads) has no golden/equiv coverage. **⚠️ EXECUTED 2026-07-22 — the headline finding is NOT-A-BUG.** §4.1 on the real corpus: `normalizeFile` (not `adaptEnvelopeNode`) is the entry point and routes **every** OxyDex shape to `adaptOxyDex` before the generic branch is reachable — the envelope always carries `nights`, a bare night always carries `hr_spikes`. All **7** corpus exports take the rich path with a full 8-key summary + events; the @907-vs-@976 ordering compared two different functions, and `dex-tests.js:12371` is a meaningful assertion (`odi4 = 3.2`), not a vacuous one. **Two live defects were fixed instead:** `hypoxicBurden` read OxyDex's internal `n.hb` while the export renames it (`oxydex-dsp.js:5712`) so it was null on every night (now 0.9–18 %·min/h); and `rmssd1Hz`/`hrVarSd1Hz` existed only on the unreachable branch, leaving OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING §Phase 3's proxy leg dead on all 7 nights (now fires on 5, null on the 2 lacking source `hrv.rmssd`). 9-assertion anti-vacuity gate; §5 still open. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | What the adapter-gap execution did **not** close. §1 the parent's §5 is untouched — the Integrator-facing PpgDex **rich** export still has no committed golden, so the whole §Phase 2–4 wiring is pinned by no fixture. §2 `hrv.rmssd` is null at source on 2 of 7 corpus nights — honest, but *why* is unexamined (legitimate gate or silent failure? record the reason in the export). §3 the `n.hb` defect was structural — the Integrator read an **internal** key the export renames — so audit **every** key `adaptOxyDex` reads against what the export builder emits, with a source-present ⇒ adapted-non-null assertion per field; a field always null across the corpus should RED, not shrug. §4 the generic `node === 'OxyDex'` branch is now unreachable-by-construction — decide deliberately between keeping it as a gated fallback or deleting it (an untested fallback is the worst option). §5 no fixture anywhere re-runs the Integrator's fusion against a real multi-node night (the `integrator_fusion_*` pair is `historical: true`, byte-pinned only).  **§1 CLOSED 2026-08-01 — the Integrator-facing RICH export now has a committed golden.** Every prior PpgDex golden was the LIGHT export (`compute({text})` emits `recording` + `ganglior_events` and nothing else), so `hrv.time.*`, `apnea.cvhrIndex` and `recording.site` — the fields `adaptPpgDex` actually reads — reproduced byte-identically on all three, because none contained them. `synthetic_ppgdex_rich_golden.node-export.json` is minted from the SAME committed input as the clean twin, so the pair isolates `opts.rich` alone; 12 legs cover byte-equivalence, anti-vacuity (each read field present and typed — `cvhrIndex` a number, since 0 is a measurement and null is not) and a control proving the light export carries none of it. Suppressing `if (opts.rich)` reds 10 of 12. GATE B now covers 15 fixtures. **§2 ANSWERED 2026-08-01 — the exports are STALE, which was neither hypothesis.** `OxyDex_2026-07-02_2205` carries `hrv: null` with `hrSamplesCleaned: 22083` (essentially every HR sample flagged, emptying `computeHRV`'s clean filter and tripping its `n < 120` floor) — so a gate DID fire, but re-running today's code on the file that export NAMES gives `hrv.rmssd` **0.5** over 22,013 samples, and `stats.minSpo2` **84 → 87**: the staleness is not confined to a null, a consumer sees a nadir 3 points too low. Generated 2026-07-03, never regenerated. The second "null" night is not a null at all — `oxydex-2026-06-12` is a reduced export SHAPE with no `hrv` key, so one symptom hid two unrelated causes. **The prescribed fix (a gated-vs-missing reason field) would not have helped**: the export was correct when written; what a consumer cannot tell is that the code that wrote it is gone. Ships `tools/oxydex-export-staleness.mjs` instead — re-runs each export against its own named source, exits 1. The class: `uploads/` exports are **gitignored working artifacts outside every gate**, the same unpinned-input-behind-a-computed-claim shape as PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01)* |
| [`PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Stop *selecting* a PPG channel and start *combining* them.** Lee'18 benchmarked our exact approach (*"multichannel best signal selection"*) against SVD fusion — **fusion won at every intensity**. Measured on a real corpus night: the 3 Verity channels are the **same wavelength at different positions** (DC spread 3.38%, near-identical perfusion, **cross-correlation lag 0 ms**, r 0.62–0.68) — spatial diversity, so combining is well-posed and the "switching injects a timing step" objection is **disproven**. `ambient` is parsed and **never used** (the SDK says subtract it). The current selector scores **one ~90 s window from the middle of the record** — ~0.25% of a 10 h night choosing for 100% of it. **Fusion benchmarks 4.6× CHEAPER than doing selection properly** (5.60 vs 25.62 ms/window) and collapses 3 detect runs into 1; weights rotate smoothly so no hysteresis is needed. Keeps consensus for beat *presence* (measured: independent noise → 68% agreement, 416 spurious beats dropped). §4's **degenerate-channel guard is EXECUTED** (2026-07-18) — closes `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` §1.3 at the DSP tier and stopped the replicated O2Ring pleth reporting a fabricated `ledAgreement 100` at `measured` tier; that half **was** export-inert (real-corpus equiv fixture reproduced byte-identical). §6 forbids selecting by ECG agreement (selection on the dependent variable — would drive the TCH ρ toward 1). The **unexecuted** Phases 1–2 (ambient subtraction + combining) remain **NOT export-inert** — they move every PpgDex export, fixture regen + computeHash owed. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-18)* |
| [`PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **The PPG algorithm settled against 20 nights of paired chest-ECG ground truth**, plus the manufacturer's own hardware/SDK record and the peer literature. Hardware is closed: **3 green LED *pairs* → ONE photodiode** (Polar staff #52/#188/#445/#671; FCC `INW4J` internal photos; patents "preferably 520 nm") — so SpO₂/CHROM/POS/multi-wavelength ICA and a 3-LED three-cornered hat are **permanently impossible**, not merely unpromising. Ambient is a **raw LEDs-off slot, NOT pre-subtracted** (unity-gain transfer 1.008–1.011 @ coherence 0.96 on a real flicker episode). Measured baseline: beat sensitivity **1.0000**, PPV **1.0000**, PPI jitter **5.92 ms**, rMSSD **+4.24 %**. Central result: **rMSSD error IS fiducial jitter** — `rMSSD²_ppg = rMSSD²_ecg + k·σ²` at per-night **R²=0.964, k=2.39** — so accuracy collapses to one scalar and the budget inverts in closed form (σ≤3.51 ms → 1 %). **Refutes by measurement:** waveform fusion of the 3 LEDs (0.95–0.97× out-of-sample; the √3 argument dies because noise-band ρ=0.94–0.99 ⇒ 0.16 dB not 4.77), overnight ambient subtraction/SQI (ambient std is a hardware constant 34.66–35.16 across all 53 non-daylight sessions), and changing the fiducial (Peralta'19's nM ranking **inverts** here: shipped tangent 4.44 ms beats nM 5.02, n′A 6.44, peak 38.0). Ranked 12-item change list — top three: **`filtfilt` has no padding** (repairs 5 surfaced numbers), **`correctRR` fabricates intervals** instead of excluding them (~29 % of the exported series at the file's own correction rate), and a **2-line `multiSession`/`multiNight` shape bug silently dropping every PpgDex multi-session event**. Refutes Phases 1–2 of `PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION`. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-21)* |
| [`O2RING-PPG-GAP-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-PPG-GAP-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **The charter for the `O2RING-PPG-GAP §N` refs four source files already cite** — honest handling of lost time + held samples on the O2Ring finger pleth, a reference the repo pointed at for weeks with no document. **§1** (PR #352): capture inserts honest gaps instead of compressing lost wall-clock. **§2** (PR #355): `intervalsSpanningTimeGap` excludes any foot-to-foot interval straddling a gap — never corrected to a plausible value. **§3 EXECUTED here:** foot-anchor `gapBeats` — the guard window was the whole systolic upstroke, so a `156` sentinel *anywhere* up to the peak condemned the beat (727/~3350 = 6.9 % deleted, then median-filled) even though only the **foot** carries PPI timing; narrowed to `foot±3`. **Validated against a paired H10 chest ECG** on a real 2026-07-20 finger night: correctRR fill 34.2 %→12 %, **0 beats lost**, per-epoch HRV moving TOWARD ECG truth (clean-sleep RMSSD error <1 ms; a no-op on clean sleep — bites only on lossy epochs). Foot-vs-peak distinction pinned directly on the now-exported `gapBeats`; 0 committed goldens moved (Verity path has `gap===null`); PpgDex + orchestrators re-bundled, GATE A/B PASS, suite 3677 green. **§4 PROPOSED (deferred):** the WIP's bridged-interval exclusion — **UNBLOCKED 2026-08-01 (§4a): that deferral watched a counter that cannot respond.** It rested on `nGapSpanIntervals: 0 → 0`, but that counts intervals straddling a **time discontinuity in the source**; a beat dropped by `gapBeats` leaves **no discontinuity in `relSec`** (every sample is still present, only the beat is gone), so `spansGap` is blind to it *by construction*. The real trigger is **`nGapBeats`**. Measured with the committed `tools/ppg-gap-bridge-scan.mjs` (drives the shipped `parsePPG` + `analyze`) over the 22 largest O2Ring finger captures: **14 of 22 files drop ≥1 beat**, **88** dropped in total, max **25** in one file — §4's Done-when was already satisfied fourteen times over when the item was parked. Even §2's own counter is non-zero on **11 of 22**, so the *0 → 0* observation came from a narrower sample than the corpus offers. A committed adversarial twin (`--selftest`) plants `156` sentinel runs **straddling a foot** and separates the two counters (`nGapBeats > 0` while `nGapSpanIntervals === 0`) with no corpus; placement is load-bearing — a first draft planted them mid-rise, clear of the foot, and would have *confirmed* the deferral it disproves (`gapBeats` fires only within ±3 samples of the FOOT). **Still NOT landed, deliberately**: §4 touches HRV and owes §5's paired-ECG per-epoch validation on those 14 files — the blocker was wrong, the validation is not optional. The `verifiedUnder` re-stamp Done-when is now **done** (PR #670). Validation method (§5): per-epoch HRV agreement, not cross-modality beat matching. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01 — §1/§2/§3 shipped; §4 unblocked, not landed)* |
| [`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Ingest the O2Ring finger-site PPG into PpgDex** — a second optical site through the SAME beat/foot/PPI/HRV/morphology pipeline the Verity wrist PPG uses. PpgDex **already** computes the full fiducial set (`ppgdex-morph.js`: foot · systolic · dicrotic notch · AI · reflection index · SDPPG b/a · Takazawa aging index); this adds a **single-channel finger parser + detection path** for the O2Ring pleth (single-ch, 8-bit u8, ~125 Hz, spike-sample rejection). Three real differences handled: no 3-LED consensus vote (one-channel fallback, `ledAgreement=null` not fake-100 %), coarse 8-bit (harder SQI gate), raw spike samples. Finger-site morphology enters **experimental** (below the Verity site's `emerging` — grade re-earned, not inherited). Committed synthetic fixture + equiv leg; DSP change → re-bundle PpgDex + GATE A/B + full suite + changeset. **Unblocks** the finger leg of `INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR`. **§3/§4/§5 SHIPPED 2026-07-19** — 1-vs-3 column resolution + `site` tag, sentinel rejected by ISOLATION not value (a trend-consistent 156 is real signal), gaps drop beats rather than being filled, `beatConfidenceAxis` replaces the absent vote, site-scoped `experimental` registry ids. 48 assertions, mutation-verified 4 ways; Verity path proven byte-identical on 4 real nights. **DONE 2026-07-20** — the §6 round-trip is VERIFIED ON REAL HARDWARE: O2Ring→PpgDex 56.3 bpm vs the ring's 1 Hz field 57.0 (Δ0.7) vs the paired H10 ECG 56.1 (Δ0.2), 313 feet, PPI foot-to-foot (`docs/O2RING-FINGER-ROUNDTRIP-2026-07-20.md`). Follow-ups in `PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **What executing the finger-site brief surfaced.** The §6 round-trip is closed on real hardware, so nothing here blocks the shipped path: (1) one belt-and-suspenders run to see `site='finger'` end-to-end on a fresh POST-#276 1-column capture (the round-trip that closed §6 used a pre-#276 replicated file that the degenerate-guard collapses to the honest single-channel path — proven identical, but tags `wrist`); (2) record the real-capture `156` sentinel rejected-vs-kept split to confirm the ~93%/7% ratio at scale. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20 — both items closed: `site='finger'` proven end-to-end on a fresh 1-column capture, and the §2.4 sentinel path exercised on real data)* |
| [`INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Promote PAT/PTT into a beat-level Integrator Vascular (trend-only) fusion layer** — lift the DONE `PAT-FEASIBILITY` coupler (`pat-feasibility.js`, `ECGDSP`×`PPGDSP`) into `integrator-dsp.js`, and add **dual-site PAT** (one R-peak → finger + wrist feet, difference cancels the pre-ejection-period confound). **Framed honestly:** the blocker was never the sensor but **~48 ppm inter-device clock drift** (swamps PAT ~24×); the feasibility brief named **single-host synchronised capture** as the unblock, and the Tepna daemon (`CLOCK_MONOTONIC`-anchored, all 3 streams on one host) **is** that path. **Phase 0 = re-run the go/no-go gate** on a single-host night (bar: drift ≤ 60 ms, coupling ≥ 55 %, IQR ≤ 60 ms) — caveat: O2Ring pleth is **host-arrival back-timed**, so BLE jitter replaces crystal drift as the residual; kill-criterion routes to `POLAR-SDK-CAPTURE` if it fails. Phase 1 consumes node exports (adds the missing **ECGDex R-peak series**). **Surfaces a Vascular trend only — NEVER a mmHg BP number** (`experimental`; cuffless BP degrades in older/hypertensive, n=3077). Cites Ding'17, Ganti'20, Wong'24, Liu'23, Charlton'21. Re-bundle Integrator/ECGDex + gates + changeset. **PHASE 0 RUN 2026-07-29 — NO-GO, and the framing above is REFUTED on two counts (§2-RESULT, §2-RESULT-II).** (1) The **~48 ppm drift premise is wrong**: 1147 ms is one RR interval, and the "drift" the gate reported was **beat-slip** from a coupler that searched 2000 ms — more than one RR — while only *displaying* its 200–650 ms window; extracted and fixed as `pat-align.js` `coupleRtoFoot`, 16 gated assertions incl. a mutation control. (2) **Single-host capture is indistinguishable from phone-stamped** (coupling 18.8 % vs 19.0 % offset-free), so the `POLAR-SDK-CAPTURE` routing is **withdrawn, not pending** — better timestamps cannot move this gate. Re-measured offset-free: **0 of 54 pairings clear**; `halfDrift` passes **47/54** (median 19.7 ms) so drift was never the blocker; the real limit is **~96 ms beat-to-beat scatter** vs a 60 ms bar, and the closest night misses by 4 pp of coupling. **No Vascular panel built**; reviving needs a beat-*correspondence* audit (§2-RESULT-II.4), since `feet/R`≈1.0 refutes net dropout but not local insertion/deletion pairs | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-18 — Phase 0 NO-GO 2026-07-29, parked)* |
| [`TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **The three-cornered hat, measured against a TRUE reference for the first time.** The σ programme exists *because* there is no reference — so nobody could ever check the estimator. CPAP's PLD channel writes **measured respiration from a calibrated flow sensor**, so on the quad-modal nights there finally is one. Driven against it (67 epochs, 11 nights), TCH fails **two ways that are invisible without truth**: it is **blind to bias by construction** (ECG under-reads respiration by **1.35 br/min** — no reference-free method or artifact gate can ever see this), and its **independence assumption is violated** (ρ = **0.42** between the ECG and PPG error terms, because both read the *same* RSA proxy with the *same* estimator) — so it rates a calibrated flow sensor as noisy as an RSA-derived estimate. The repo's correlated solve **cannot** fix it: its `rho` is one **common-mode** term across all three pairs, not a pairwise structure. **Complements** `TRIO-ARTIFACT-GATE` (that brief found TCH is not robust to *artifact*, diagnosed by implausibility; this one has *truth*) — clean the epochs and both defects remain. Also finds two F1-class defects: `PPGDSP.lombScargle` never tracks the HF peak (hence `respRate: null`), and ECGDex computes respiration and exports neither estimate | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-07-12)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md) | **First whole-SD-card run of CPAPDex** — ~180 real nights (1139 EDF, 222 sessions) driven through `CPAPDex.compute()`, 39 paired with O2Ring, **17 quad-modal** (CPAP+ECG+PPG+O2Ring). **Code-only by design** (the clinical read stays in a local gitignored log). Six defects synthetic fixtures cannot find: **the node-export drops 17 of 32 computed metrics** incl. the *entire* ventilation + flow-limitation lane (15 populated on ≥168/180 nights — and the dropped lane holds the corpus's strongest event-burden predictor, detrended r≈0.5, vs r≈0.16 for the pressure/leak fields we *do* export); the `mode` heuristic flips 57× on a bare `pressIqr>1.0` cut; **CPAPDex's "can't join the equivalence gate" blocker is FALSE** (binary multi-file EDF runs headless in Node — closes the fleet's last synthetic-only GATE-C, but the raw EDF headers carry a device identifier that must be scrubbed or synthesized around first); no ResMed ingest adapter; `buildLongitudinal` returns `crossNight:null` **silently** in any Node realm (inconsistent dep resolution); SA2 lane 0/222 real coverage. Proposes a shuffled-null **cross-node event-coupling primitive** (a chance baseline *inverts* the naive co-occurrence conclusion) and **CPAP flow as a respiration REFERENCE to validate the reference-free σ/TCH estimator** — the one experiment that programme can never otherwise run | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-12 — **P1 · P2 · P9 EXECUTED** (ventilation lane on the bus 15→30 metrics; binary-EDF equiv leg via a **committed synthetic** EDF set, no device serial published; and the discovery that **every** node's `compute() ≡ export` leg had been SKIPPING in CI because each input was a gitignored real recording — now every node has a committed synthetic input, so the suite's strongest correctness gate finally executes). **§P5 EXECUTED** — `event-coupling.js`, the shuffled-null coupling primitive, whose construction found **three defects in the prototype §M1's own numbers came from**, all biased toward believing: the null shift did not **wrap** (surrogates fell off the recording end → chance deflated → **lift inflated**, i.e. couplings manufactured); a **saturated** window (wider than B's inter-event interval) crushes lift to ~1.0 *by arithmetic* and was indistinguishable from "no coupling" (now flagged via the exact ceiling `maxLift = 100/chancePct`); and the whole-minute default shifts **resonate** with any round periodicity — a planted, *perfect* coupling scored **lift 1.006**, a false negative. §M1's conclusions survive; its magnitudes must be re-derived. **§P6 EXECUTED** → spawned `TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`: measured against a true reference for the first time, **the reference-free TCH estimator does not recover the true σ** — blind to bias, and its independence assumption fails (ρ = 0.42). **§P3 EXECUTED** — `adapters/resmed-edf.js` registered; CPAP was the fleet's only adapter-less signal type, and the premise ("EDF is binary, so no text-stream adapter") only ever ruled out a *text* adapter — it takes its bytes off the same `ctx` hatch `oxydex-spo2` already uses. The §F4 session-grouping rule is now gated **both ways**: both EVE files survive, AND the naive ±60 s-only rule *provably drops one*. **§P4 EXECUTED** — the `mode` heuristic was not badly tuned, it was **measuring the wrong thing**: the raw pressure IQR is dominated by **EPR** (a 1–3 cmH₂O drop on every *expiration*, sampled at 0.5 Hz), so a **fixed** machine with EPR=3 read "APAP". No constant could fix that. `mode` now rides a minutes-scale, EPR-immune P90 pressure envelope, **refuses to guess** (dead-band → `null`), and is **stability-guarded** at night level (every session must agree — the first session no longer names the night; the real 06-12 night now correctly reads `null`). Both surfaces shipping the old rule are gone, including a subtitle stating it to the user as fact. ⚠️ The two cut points are **not calibrated** against the ~180-night corpus (not in-repo) — the dead-band makes that safe, and calibration is carried in the follow-up)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **What executing `CPAP-REAL-CORPUS` surfaced and did not close.** The `mode` estimator is now right but its **two cut points are set from physiology, not fitted to the corpus** (the fixtures agree — real nights 1.2/2.2, synthetic 1.08, fixed machine ~0 — but 1.08 sits uncomfortably close to the 1.0 cut; the dead-band makes the failure mode `null` rather than a wrong device setting, so this is an *unmeasured* gap, not a bug). `event-coupling.js` is gated but **dormant — no node consumes it yet**, and §M1's *magnitudes* were computed with the non-wrapping null and must be **re-derived** before publication (its conclusions survive). **`Dex-Test-Suite.html?full` has not been run since P4**, which changed two rendered surfaces. P7 (apnea→CVHR coupling on the quad-modal nights) and P8 (A4's dated step-changes as the first real test of `CPAPCross` change detection, which has only ever run on `sd:0` 'stable' synthetics) were never in the parent's Done-when and are now cheaper. Also: `build.mjs` re-stamps a fixture's `manifestHash` but does **not** recompute its `outputHash` — a fleet-wide gap `tools/regen-cpap-goldens.mjs` closes for CPAP only | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-12)* |
| [`EVENT-COUPLING-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EVENT-COUPLING-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md) | **The `event-coupling.js` primitive — its contract, and the FOUR ways its null model lied.** The suite's "is it real or coincidence?" test: two frequent event streams co-occur *by construction*, so a raw co-occurrence rate is not evidence of anything — `coupling()` measures it against circular time-shift surrogates. Written up separately because the primitive was born inside a *CPAP* brief and corrected twice more in a CPAP *follow-up*, so its hard-won guards lived scattered across two documents about a subject it has nothing to do with — while the module still has **no node consumer**. **Every guard exists because it was learned the hard way, on real data, after the module shipped and its numbers had already been written up as findings:** a **non-wrapping shift** INFLATED lift (invented a ×3.3–10 coupling); **no coverage model** DEFLATED it (30% of apneas happened while the oximeter was *not recording*, and were scored as MISSES → manufactured *anti*-coupling at ×0.72); **no power floor** made a 16-event bucket look decisive (a ×0.0 published as *"provably no signal"* — chance alone predicts 0.9 hits there, so zero is a ~41% outcome); and **resonant whole-minute shifts** collapsed a planted, *perfect* coupling to ×1.006. THE RULE: read `lift` only when `underpowered` and `saturated` are both false. The synthetic self-tests were green through all four | Brief *(REFERENCE — living)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md) | **What the CPAP briefs left open — and one false DONE.** Opens by correcting itself: `CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS` was stamped **DONE while its §5/§6 had never been executed** — *the same defect those briefs spent their length closing* (a claim made, nothing verifying it; the `docs-ledger` gate checks a status header's **format**, not its **truth**, exactly as GATE B checked a fixture's bytes but not whether anything reproduced them). The unexecuted work is carried out here so the parent's DONE becomes true. Open: **P7** — `event-coupling.js` is shipped, gated and **dormant** (no node consumes it, so the Integrator's "is it real?" question is still being answered by raw co-occurrence somewhere); **P8** — **`CPAPCross` has never been shown to detect a change at all** (it only ever runs on synthetic nights with `sd:0` and a `'stable'` label, while A4's two *dated* device-setting step-changes sit unused as labelled ground truth); a **demo-input gate** (nothing stops another node repeating the CPAPDex demo that had never worked on any fresh clone); and the **generated-list merge tax** (`changes-list.txt`/`docs-ledger-list.txt` are committed snapshots of the filesystem that every PR regenerates, so they conflict *by construction* — four rebases on one PR; they exist only because the browser lane can't list a directory, so make those gates Node-only and delete them) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-III-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-III-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **What closing §P8 surfaced, plus the §5 residue.** -II shipped `CPAPCross.pressureChangePoints()` — a cross-night device-SETTING change-point detector (robust L1-cost binary segmentation), validated on the real 180-night corpus: it flags the #151 epap95 step (10.7→6.8) with zero false positives and **honestly returns empty on the noise-dominated pressureEnvIqr**, correcting the brief's assumed #169 to *not a robust change-point*. The detector + additive `crossNight.pressureChangePoints` export field are live/gated. Carried here: **§1** a user-facing render banner for the change-points (deferred — opens the evidence-badge COVERAGE MANDATE + cohesion surface); **§2** apnea→**motion-arousal** coupling (the second `event-coupling` pairing -II §P7 left unwired); **§3** generalize the per-node goldens regen tools into `regen-goldens.mjs --node` (CPAP just gained the `outputHash` re-record they already had); **§4** `how-to-collect/resmed-edf.md` + the `pressureRange goodDirection` vocabulary question. **EXECUTED 2026-07-17:** §1 render banner shipped (badged, export-inert), §3 unified into `regen-goldens.mjs --node` over a shared core, §4 resolved (adapter id named; pressureRange/mode left with reasons); §2 RE-DEFERRED — no motion-arousal event stream exists to couple against (needs an ACC-derived arousal source first) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17)* |
| [`ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **Five maintainability wins after the checkJs type gate** — a phase-per-PR plan written for a local/cheaper model. **P1** convert the brittle source-text safety gates (regex-slicing a function body out of `tests/dex-tests.js`) to behavioral tests that call the function and assert the effect (strictly stronger; unblocks P4). **P2** turn on `strictNullChecks` one gated module at a time (fix the null/`{}`/`never` class at source, not with casts). **P3** split the single `BUILD-MANIFEST.json`/`FIXTURE-PROVENANCE.json` into per-app ledger fragments so parallel bundle PRs stop colliding (the §👥.3 "one session at a time" bottleneck). **P4** (after P1) reflow the whole tree and retire `biome.json`'s formatter-override list. **P5** ESM for the node realms — DEFERRED / research-only (the type gate already made the DSP↔render coupling visible, capturing much of the benefit). Ships no runtime change; includes an environment + gate-command cheat-sheet and the hard-won lessons from PRs #70–#79 | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-15 — P1·P2·P3·P4 merged; P5 deferred)* |
| [`ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md) | **Follow-ups surfaced executing P4 (whole-tree Biome reflow).** **F1** (required before next release) — the reflow rewrote DSP text inside every app's compute closure, so `computeHash` moved and `verifiedUnder` on the 14 corpus-backed fixtures is stale; re-stamp with `DEX_UPLOADS=<full-corpus> node tools/verify-fixtures.mjs` (fail-closed in a partial-corpus env, hence owner action; `release.mjs` blocks until done). Export bytes are provably unchanged (0 output/input hashes moved), so no merge gate reds. **F2** process note — a reflow-safe gate audit must test source-mirror gates against reflowed bytes for files CI never format-checked (P4-prep's blind spot; ~14 gate groups hardened in the P4 PR). **F3** watch-out — Biome 2.5.3 formatter is non-idempotent on some JSDoc-cast constructs (needs a 2nd pass to the fixpoint) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17 — F1 discharged: all 14 fixtures verified under the current compute closure, `verify-fixtures --check` = 0 unverified; F2/F3 no code owed)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md) | **Make the DSP↔render coupling a real module boundary — spike first, decide, then fan out.** The executable spin-out of `ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION` **P5** (which stays deferred there). Each node is 4–5 plain global scripts sharing page scope; real `import`/`export` would delete the `<node>-globals.d.ts` scaffolding and the source-mirror gates that P4's reflow kept breaking. **Phase 0 is a throwaway inliner SPIKE on GlucoDex** (smallest node, no worker), behind a `--esm` flag writing a scratch `GlucoDex.esm.html`: prove the owned build can bundle ES-module source into ONE offline `Foo.html` that passes `no-network` + GATE A/B and boots under `file://` (the one cost local horsepower can't buy down — it's architectural). Everything after Phase 0 is CONDITIONAL on a GO. Written for a **local Ubuntu coder with the full corpus + headless Chromium** (runs the browser lane the cloud env can't). PpgDex's worker blob converts last. **Phase 0 (spike) → GO; Phase 1 (GlucoDex UI modules → ESM via `build-core.js esmBundle`) + Phase 2 (GlucoDex DSP → dual-mode ESM + the `classicify` co-load bridge for the orchestrators + both test runners) executed 2026-07-15**, export-inert by verification. **Phase 3 fan-out COMPLETE (all 7 nodes ESM) + Phase 4 executed in the owner-scoped middle path, 2026-07-16** (see `ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16`, DONE). **DONE 2026-07-23** — the remaining Done-when items 4–5 executed via `ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II`: DSP→UI reach-ins inverted to dependency injection (export-inert), the 7 node `<node>-globals.d.ts` consolidated to one `node-globals.d.ts`, the `LoadOwnExport` attach-marker source-mirror gates already retired + the reach-in allow-list gate behavioralized; PRs #370/#372/#373; `ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION §P5` flipped DONE-via-this-brief. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-23)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **The ESM-migration fleet fan-out — originally parked, then funded: ALL 7 NODES DONE; only Phase 4 remains.** Spawned after Phases 0–2 landed (GlucoDex fully ESM + the reusable `esmBundle`/`classicify` bridge). Phase 3 is NOT the mechanical sweep the parent anticipated: each remaining node has a structural blocker (indirect DSP consumption / no clean import edge in oxydex·hrvdex·pulsedex; no-IIFE `render`/`overview` in pulsedex; a Web-Worker blob in **ecgdex AND ppgdex**; edf/fusion/cross + non-orchestrator co-load in cpapdex; a load-order hazard making any DSP-only conversion a dead-export churn or a risky UI change). And the P5 payoff (delete `-globals.d.ts`, retire source-mirror gates) only lands once the fan-out is COMPLETE and the classic co-load path is retired — a partial fleet unlocks nothing. Captures the per-node blockers + the full-GlucoDex-treatment completion recipe. **Execution 2026-07-16: nodes 2–4 landed** (CPAPDex — the CJS `--selftest` path dropped; ECGDex — hermetic worker blob; PpgDex — toString-serialized pool worker), one node per fully-gated commit, plus fleet infrastructure: the worker co-load bridge in all five workers (repairing a latent `cohort-worker.js` GlucoDex regression) and the `browser-gates.mjs` timeout fix. **Execution II (same day): the deep 3 landed** — PulseDex set the template (window-publish blocks + defineProperty proxies for shared mutable state + side-effect imports), HRVDex applied it, OxyDex's 'hardest' DSP turned out IIFE-wrapped all along (two real bugs caught: the `var UP` non-configurable window property; a sixth un-bridged worker). **Phase 4 executed 2026-07-16 (owner-scoped middle path)**: app pages namespaced + explicit `_bare` helper-imports + mutable-state proxies — the import boundary is real and self-verifying (a missed helper throws); the spray stays as a documented test/worker-realm surface. The full-ambition remainder is scoped+parked in FOLLOWUPS-II. Brief DONE. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-16)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **What the Phase-4 middle path deliberately left open — measured and priced.** The rest of the original P5 ambition, scoped from real counts: (1) rewrite `tests/dex-tests.js`'s hundreds of deliberate bare-helper calls (`rmssd`×116, `parseTimestamp`×26 …) to namespace form — only then can the spray blocks be deleted; (2) qualify the six workers' ~20 bare calls; (3) invert the DSP→UI reach-ins (`setStatus`/`rerender`/`getProfile`… — dependency injection, a per-DSP behavioral refactor); (4) delete the seven `<node>-globals.d.ts` (unlocked by 3); (5) behavioralize the P4-hardened source-mirror gates; (6) then flip ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION §P5 + the parent brief DONE. Also records: Integrator + the orchestrator app layers are NOT ESM (fresh decision, not a leftover); the `<Node>` namespace attaches are permanent public API, not debt. Recommendation: stay parked — the middle path already bought the verified app-page boundary; this buys type-hygiene, not correctness. **DONE 2026-07-23 — all six items EXECUTED** (reach-in DI PRs #370/#372; d.ts consolidation PR #373; source-mirror marker gates already retired + reach-in gate behavioralized; parent + §P5 flipped DONE #374; **items 1–2 spray deletion** — the estimate was stale, the spray survived only on hrvdex/pulsedex/oxydex-dsp serving the workers, so cohort-worker was namespaced to pull helpers from `<Node>._bare` and the three spray blocks deleted, export-inert). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-23)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-SPIKE-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-SPIKE-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md) | **Phase 0 inliner-spike findings for the ESM migration — verdict GO.** The throwaway go/no-go spike for `ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`, run locally on GlucoDex with headless Chromium + the committed synthetic corpus, fully non-destructive. Proves the owned inliner CAN bundle ES-module source into one offline plain-inline `Foo.html`: a dependency-free module-registry bundler → one classic `<script>`, then `build-core.js` inlines it — **16 assets, zero network edges, real non-null manifestHash `023172618bf1`**. Boots under `file://` **and** `http://` with **0 errors/0 CSP violations**, full graph wired in-browser, `compute()` gives a real summary; export is **byte-identical ESM ≡ classic ≡ committed golden** on both inputs. Per-node edit is tiny (~23 changed lines; IIFEs stay intact, only ~7 inter-node symbols become `import`/`export`). Confirms inline modules boot offline but external `import` fails under `file://` (the CORS wall — why inlining is mandatory). Next likely go/no-go is Phase 2 (the co-load contract), not an inliner wall. **STOP for owner GO before Phase 1** | Findings *(REFERENCE 2026-07-15)* |
| [`LOCAL-DOWNLOAD-FILE-URL-FIX-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/LOCAL-DOWNLOAD-FILE-URL-FIX-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **The science tools now run from a local download (file://), like the apps.** A user downloaded a "trio sigma" tool to disk and it did nothing — it only worked when served. Confirmed with headless Chromium under `file://`: the analysis tools (unlike the bundled apps) loaded logic from external `<script src>` siblings and spun `new Worker('x.js')`, which the browser **forbids under `file://`** (opaque origin → `SecurityError` on the worker; `Failed to fetch` on the data path) — so a downloaded shell had no compute. New **`tools/build-analysis.mjs`** bundles all 9 tools to self-contained single-file HTML: inline every script, and rewrite `new Worker('w.js')` → a **blob-URL worker** whose importScripts deps are inlined behind a hoisted DOM/window shim (mirrors `cohort-worker.js`). CSP unchanged (once inline, `'unsafe-inline'` + `blob:` cover it — same as the apps). Verified under file:// **and** http://: 0 page errors, every worker completes `init`→`ready`. Gated two ways: `build-analysis.mjs --check` in CI (staleness — the tools inline DSP code) + a Node-suite invariant group (no external `<script src>`, no `new Worker('file')`) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-14)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md) | **`AUDIT-PROMPT.md` executed end-to-end — 21 reproducible defects, 11 of which mis-state a number the user sees.** Both gates were green before *and* after: these live in the space the gates don't cover. Headline: the **Clock Contract §3 file-level DMY/MDY lock is not implemented** (`clock.js:40` decides per-row) → an MDY-configured O2Ring night runs time *backward* → `durationMin = −254460`, **ODI-4 = 0/h, hypoxic burden = 0 — an apnea night reads perfectly healthy**; a Polar H10 **accelerometer** file is analyzed as a heart recording (HR 61.9 bpm, stress 100, 36 events — a gravity rail read as RR); HRVDex **`HF n.u.` = 125 000 000 %** (a 0–100 quantity) and **`MxDMn/MeanRR` 1000× low** (the un-guarded sibling of `guardBaevsky`); GlucoDex counts **gap-fill interpolation as measured glucose** (TIR 11 % vs a true 0 %) and **misses the real Lingo clip floor** (37 `nocturnal_hypo` @conf 0.97 off a 54 mg/dL rail); OxyDex's **REM estimate is impossible on 39/39 real nights** (median 77.5 %, coloured "good"); the Integrator says **"Sources agree … reliable"** when nothing was comparable, publishes a **glucose⟷autonomic coupling with no glucose in it**, **can never fuse HRVDex**, and its **kernel-drift audit is blind to 3 of 7 nodes**. **Why the gates missed it:** every `env.equiv` fixture is a *clean ~6-min clip*, so the long/lossy/differently-configured failures are structurally invisible. Also records the verified **clean bills** — the charter's own flagship ECG-transient suspicion is **refuted**, the Lomb–Scargle path is real (planted 800 ms² tone → 799), the ÷N-vs-÷N−1 SDNN bug stays fixed, and viewer-timezone independence holds | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-07-12 · all 21 findings executed.** §1 Clock DMY/MDY file-lock · §2 ACC-as-RR foreign-stream veto · §3/§4 HRVDex spectral presence-gates + Baevsky unit guard · §5/§6 GlucoDex gap-fill + the real Lingo clip floor · §7/§8/§9 OxyDex real event clock, whole-night windows, REM plausibility · §12–§16 Integrator read-set + kernel audit · §10/§11 one spectral time scale, no grid lottery · §17–§21 hygiene (export naming, null-not-zero, **profile origins — a population default no longer ships as your own data**, pooled ODI/T90, badge coverage incl. `.ev-corner`, which the mandate prescribed but the engine never defined). Every fix gated by a group **verified to red on the original code**. Final: headless 2125/2125 · GATE A 8/8 · GATE B green · `build --check` clean. Follow-up: `DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`)* |
| [`FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **A fixture may not claim reproducibility nobody checked.** Specs the gate for the 2026-07-14 miss: `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14 §1` moved GlucoDex's real export, the equiv leg that would have caught it **SKIPPED** (its input is a gitignored recording — absent in CI *and* on the executing session's machine), GATE B stayed green (it is static, it never re-runs the app), and **`build.mjs` re-stamped the fixture's `manifestHash`** — auto-authoring a reproducibility claim no gate had tested. Every gate green, brief DONE, release shipped, and the **served GlucoDex ran the pre-fix DSP against real users** until it was caught by hand. The defect is not "a leg skipped" but that *the skip leaves a false claim behind, written by a tool*. Four parts: **§1 `computeHash`** — a `manifestHash`-style projection restricted to the export's **compute closure** (the module set a headless realm needs to run `compute()`; the regen tools already encode it), so **"EXPORT-INERT" stops being a claim in a commit message and becomes a computed value** — render edits move `manifestHash` but not `computeHash`; a DSP edit moves both. **§2 `verifiedUnder`** — the fixture records the code that *actually re-ran the app and reproduced these bytes*; `build.mjs` is **forbidden** to write it (only the regen tools + a new `verify-fixtures.mjs` may), so the ledger can no longer assert what nobody checked. **§3** three placements: the suite *names* every UNVERIFIED fixture (a skip stops being silent) · **`release.mjs` refuses to cut a release with one** (the wall sits where harm materializes, and where the owner *has* the corpus — this alone would have blocked v1.10.1) · CI red, sequenced LAST (a corpus-less contributor cannot green it). **§4** finishes `DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12 §A`, which established committed-synthetic adversarial twins and shipped four for OxyDex but **left GlucoDex with none** — a 14 h-gap synthetic Lingo would have reddened this in CI, on the PR, with no corpus. Migration **verifies once on the corpus, then stamps — never back-fills** (that would fabricate the very claim being abolished); **that pass has been RUN (2026-07-14, v1.10.2): every corpus-backed leg reproduces — GlucoDex was the only stale one**, so the gate lands green rather than reding on arrival | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-07-15** — §1 computeHash · §2 verifiedUnder · §3.1 visibility · §3.2 the release WALL · §4 twins · §5 migration all EXECUTED; re-confirmed 10/10 green post-P3. The closure shipped as a **denylist, not the allowlist the brief sketched** — an allowlist that forgets a module fails OPEN and the gate goes blind; a denylist merely over-flags. Proven on the real bundle: a render-only edit moves manifestHash but NOT computeHash (gate stays green), a DSP edit moves both (gate reds) — and it catches the §1 scenario with NO corpus. Migration re-verified + stamped all 14 corpus-backed fixtures, so the gate landed GREEN. **§3.3 (CI-red) deliberately deferred** — a corpus-less contributor cannot green it; the release wall covers the harm)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **Second deep audit — after ~15 PRs / 193 commits — with the recent churn cleared and 8 older latent defects found.** Four adversarial auditors, each verified by code trace. **The recent PR churn (checkJs / strictNullChecks casts + biome reflows) is a CLEAN BILL** — runtime-inert, GATE-B confirms no fixture moved; the findings are *residue of `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11`'s incomplete fixes* (applied at the headline, not propagated to every consumer), not regressions. Correctness-first: **§1** GlucoDex counts long sensor-gap **interpolation as measured glucose** in ~9 metric families (CONGA/MODD/GVP/AGP/per-day/nocturnal-hypo/…) because the §5 `GAP_LONG` exclusion was wired only into `analyzableIndex` — the docstring says "every distribution metric," the code excludes it from one; **§2** the Integrator **silently drops a multi-night CPAP export's entire per-night payload** (device-scored AHI vanishes from fusion, no warning — the `nights[]` unwrap is OxyDex-only); **§3** PulseDex breaks the Task-Force identity `vlf+lf+hf==totalPower` on overnight (four independent medians vs the defined sum ECGDex/PpgDex carry); **§4** GlucoDex `detectSessions` can never see a sensor change (scans `FLAG.GAP`, gaps are `GAP_LONG`); **§5** OxyDex ODI rates on two time bases (`n/3600` vs elapsed span — needs an author call on which is canonical); **§6** Integrator resurrects the retired per-session CPAP `mode` (latent); **§7** CPAPDex `periodicBreathingPct:0`-not-null on zero-duration; **§8** SD1 estimator drift (negligible). Cleared: event-coupling, EDF clock, CPAP pooling, core HRV estimators (no SDNN ÷N regression), Baevsky guards. **ALL 8 EXECUTED 2026-07-14** — §5 by owner-ratified basis decision (SAMPLE, after a 37-night corpus measurement showed the divergence is inert on real data), the rest agent-executed; each with a failing-assertion gate verified RED-first and export-inertness verified via `computeHash`/`verify-fixtures` | Brief *(**DONE 2026-07-14**)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **The residue of the deep audit, and the blind spot that hid it.** Headline (§A): **every `env.equiv.*` fixture is a clean ~6-minute clip**, which is *why* three Tier-1 findings (§1 clock lock, §8 event clock, §9 whole-night windows) were structurally invisible while both gates stayed green — the gates proved *reproducibility*, and both sides were wrong together. The fix is not another assertion but a **fixture with the failure modes in it**: a long, lossy, real night (≥6 h, `- -` no-reading rows, spanning midnight) from the 39-night O2Ring corpus. Also carries the deliberately-scoped-short work: PulseDex's two deeper fail-open layers under §2 (any column with a median in 300–2000 is accepted as RR; SDNN 9.5 ms over 3 h went unquestioned) · `_envToSeed`'s `0`-for-absent seed · the `||0` siblings of the HRV Score · the OxyDex **tail**-slice family (§9 fixed only the head-slice) · `remProxyPct` vs `remFraction` denominating on different clocks · the REM estimator itself, never re-derived · CPAPDex `apnea`/`hypopnea` feeding no fusion rule (and absent from `EVENT-LEXICON.md`) · and the process lesson from §6: **a long-standing assertion that a fixture was a "clean, unclamped file" was itself wrong** — the real Lingo export always railed at 54 mg/dL | Brief *(**IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-12** — **§A EXECUTED**: four **committed synthetic** adversarial inputs (MDY order · dropped rows · a full-length night) now run in the equiv gate as INVARIANTS, each with a control proving it bites. ⚠️ **This brief's own prescription for §A was WRONG, and the correction IS the lesson**: it asked for a *real* night — but real recordings are gitignored personal data, which is precisely *why* the GATE-C equivalence legs skip in CI. A real adversarial night would have been gitignored too: green on the maintainer's machine, CI as blind as before. The fixture had to be **synthetic so that it could be committed**. Verified in a fresh clone (what CI actually gets): all 16 asserts pass, and re-introducing the §9 head-slice reds it there. **§B EXECUTED** — an interval must CONSERVE TIME (19/19 real recordings sum to their elapsed span within 1%; the accelerometer read as RR claims 24.6×), which is a conservation law rather than the variability floor the brief prescribed — that floor would have MISSED the Verity rail entirely (its SDNN sits inside the real RR range) and risked rejecting real pathology. Absence is now null, not 0, across HRVDex seeds/siblings and OxyDex decoupling — including a fabricated green "Stress 0 · ok" the brief had assumed reached nobody. §C–§E open)* |

| [`ECG-RPEAK-SEED-FIX-2026-06-27-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECG-RPEAK-SEED-FIX-2026-06-27-BRIEF.md) | User-reported: a real overnight Polar H10 `*_ECG.txt` that OPENS mid electrode-settling threw "Too few R-peaks detected". `detectPeaks` seeded the Pan-Tompkins threshold from `max(first 2 s)` — the ~5 kµV settling transient ≫ the ~600 µV overnight QRS (~18× in the squared integrate) — and `SPKI` only decays when a peak FIRES, so once seeded high it never recovered → <12 peaks → false "flat" throw on an otherwise-good night. Fixed: robust global-percentile seed (`_seedScale`), behaviour-preserving on clean records + transient-immune; regression gate (group 12c). Re-bundled `7c625af51078→bfa1aa934fcc` (buildHash unchanged); both gates green | Brief *(DONE 2026-06-27)* |

## 📦 Releases, versioning & compliance (IEC 62304 / ISO 13485-aligned)

> One suite **SemVer** (canonical in `suite.manifest.json` `version`) is the release identity. The
> current tree is **1.0.0** (`CONTROLLED-RELEASES-2026-07-05`). *Alignment, not conformance* — Tepna
> remains "Not a medical device"; every compliance doc carries that disclaimer.

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CHANGELOG.md`](CHANGELOG.md) | The suite changelog (Keep a Changelog) — one section per controlled release; history below 1.0.0 is reconstructed from the DONE brief corpus | Reference *(living)* |
| [`RELEASE-MANIFEST.json`](RELEASE-MANIFEST.json) | Machine-readable release ledger — per release: version, date, contributing briefs, per-app `manifestHash` snapshot. The `release-ledger` gate keeps it ≡ the canonical `suite.manifest.json` version | Record *(ledger)* |
| [`SOFTWARE-LIFECYCLE-PLAN.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/SOFTWARE-LIFECYCLE-PLAN.md) | Umbrella — SemVer bump semantics + the IEC 62304 / ISO 13485 clause crosswalk (with an honest gap column) | Reference *(living)* |
| [`SAFETY-CLASSIFICATION.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/SAFETY-CLASSIFICATION.md) | IEC 62304 §4.3 software safety class — Class A rationale (non-device; retrospective descriptive summaries only) | Reference *(living)* |
| [`CONFIGURATION-MANAGEMENT-PLAN.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/CONFIGURATION-MANAGEMENT-PLAN.md) | IEC 62304 §8 — configuration items, change control, status accounting (= CHANGELOG + RELEASE-MANIFEST), reproducibility | Reference *(living)* |
| [`SOUP-LIST.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/SOUP-LIST.md) | IEC 62304 §5.3.3/§8.1.2 — runtime SOUP **empty by design**; build-time tooling only | Reference *(living)* |
| [`SOFTWARE-RELEASE-PROCEDURE.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/SOFTWARE-RELEASE-PROCEDURE.md) | IEC 62304 §5.8 release SOP — the `tools/release.mjs` runbook, sign-off, reproducibility evidence | Reference *(living)* |
| [`DOCUMENT-CONTROL.md`](docs/COMPLIANCE/DOCUMENT-CONTROL.md) | ISO 13485 §4.2.4/§4.2.5 crosswalk — the brief lifecycle + gates **as** document/record control | Reference *(living)* |

Step-by-step plan to drop `'unsafe-inline'` from `script-src` fleet-wide: a shared **event-delegation dispatcher** (`dex-actions.js`) → convert all ~150 inline `on*=` handlers (`.src.html` + runtime `innerHTML`) to `data-act` → hash the inline `<script>` blocks in the owned bundler → drop `'unsafe-inline'` + verify every app's interactions headless (+ a negative-control injection-blocked test). Makes CSP an *injection* backstop, not just the egress control it is today. Large, EXPORT-INERT. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-11)* |

Baseline `connect-src 'none'\|'self'; default-src 'self'` per page + extend `no-network.html` `PAGES` / the `tests/dex-tests.js` CSP-presence leg. **No re-bundle** (pages are outside `BUILD-MANIFEST.json`), EXPORT-INERT. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-13)* |

| [`EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-12.md`](audits/EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-12.md) | Efficiency pass per `EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md` (2026-07-12; baseline green). **The two headline results are not speedups.** **C1 (HIGH, correctness)** — `ppgdex-morph.js` steps a sample loop by a NON-INTEGER stride (`winSamp = 60 × fs`, `fs` is always a float on a real Polar file), so `raw[fractional]` → `undefined` → `NaN` → the surfaced per-window **perfusion-index trend is mostly `null`** (2 of 18 windows on a real capture); the `Math.round` fix is also **2.24×** and leaves both gated PpgDex fixtures byte-identical. **G1 (HIGH, gate integrity)** — **CI runs a WEAKER gate than local**: 2087 assertions/11 skipped vs 2107/2, and **GATE B verifies only 10 of 23 fixtures** ("uploads/ not served" — real recordings are gitignored); nothing pins the skip count, so a new skip is invisible. **M1 (HIGH, method)** — the Node `vm` realm distorts profiles up to **12.3×**, which is where `PROFILED-HOTSPOTS §3` came from: the "28% in `mean`" hoist measures **1.015×** end-to-end → **§3 RETRACTED**. Plus **P1** PpgDex parses 190k timestamps it never reads (**compute 2.01×**, byte-identical), **D1** local gate still single-threaded (102 s vs 28 s forked), **D2** the two generated list files conflict in 45% of commits and a conflicted PR gets **no CI at all**, **D3** a red gate prints 169 KB and names the failure once on line 1651, **X1–X4** `CLAUDE.md`'s `manifestHash` paragraph is stale + 4 DOCS-INDEX rows contradict their brief header. Dismissed: don't cache tsc/npm/Playwright (latency-neutral), don't shard past 4 | Audit |

| [`MOTIONDEX-RESPIRATORY-RATE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MOTIONDEX-RESPIRATORY-RATE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | Replace `respiratoryEffort()`'s zero-crossing rate (measured **MAE 3.59 br/min** vs a real CPAP-flow reference — *worse than predicting a constant*) with spectral ridge tracking (**MAE 1.01**, 91.6% within 2 br/min over 26 nights / 19,193 epochs). Ships the estimator, the `emerging` tier, and the three preprints the validation corpus unlocks (`papers/cpap-flow-reference.html`, `acc-respiratory-rate.html`, `effort-typing-null.html` — all DRAFT until the analysis tool is ported). Records two constraints on what may be claimed: a tilt-angle channel is provably redundant, and **posture robustness is untested** (corpus roll IQR 13.1–17.9°) | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-21 — estimator landed; papers blocked on the analysis-tool port)* |
| [`INTEGRATOR-APNEA-TYPING-REVIEW-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-APNEA-TYPING-REVIEW-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | `typeApneaByEffort()` types apneas from chest-ACC effort **amplitude**, which measurement shows does not carry that information: effort during central apnea sits at **0.99×** its own night baseline (not absent, *normal*), only **16.5%** fall below half baseline where a single RIP belt reaches **84%**, and best achievable discrimination is **AUC 0.691**. An absolute `EFFORT_FLOOR_G` gate therefore types 83.5–95.4% of central apneas as OBSTRUCTIVE — wrong for the dominant class. The CPAP-pressure explanation was tested against `MaskPress.2s` and **fails** (ρ = −0.174, p = 0.0008). Offers four options (recommended: abstain) and does **not** presume the owner's call. **EXECUTED 2026-07-31 — option 1, abstain:** `obstructive`/`central` are now permanently **`null`** (never `0` — a zero reads as "measured none"), every desat is `untyped` with a machine-readable `typingWithdrawn`/`withdrawnReason`, no `apnea_obstructive`/`apnea_central` impulse is emitted, and both tuning constants are **deleted** + gate-scanned so the rule cannot be re-enabled by tuning. The surviving measurement is `effortCovered` (how many desats the chest ACC witnessed). Option 3 (central-only) rejected on the same corpus: the shipped central branch fired at `frac === 0` against a floor mis-scaled 0.2×–3×, so it too asserts a type the feature does not support. §7.2 corrects this brief's own premise — **there was never a render surface** (typing shipped export-only; the typed events were built and dropped), and §7.3 records that there was likewise no evidence badge to re-tier. Gated by the rewritten `apnea-typing` group, now pinning the **negative** (effort present throughout ⇒ no type) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`MOTIONDEX-RESPIRATORY-RATE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/MOTIONDEX-RESPIRATORY-RATE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | What executing the resp-rate brief surfaced. Blocking item: re-run the 26-night corpus end-to-end through `resp-acc-analysis.html` so the papers' numbers trace to a repo tool. Records that **sample-rate precision is this codebase's recurring silent failure** (it bit three times in one work-unit — count÷duration, an assumed rate, and a millisecond-quantised stamp giving a 1.2% error → tens of minutes of clock-offset error) and proposes a shared `nativeHz` helper; plus integer-decimation-is-not-resampling, a double-filtering footgun, that the confidence gate is **not** an apnea filter, that posture is untestable on this corpus, and that the browser render-coverage gate was never run. **§2·§3·§4 GATED 2026-08-01 (§2a).** The premise was worse than stated: all three fixes existed, but `resp-acc-analysis.js` was loaded by **neither test lane**, so `nativeHz`/`toGrid`/the channel constructors had **zero** coverage and every fix rested on the comment above it — the same shape as the defect. Now wired into both runners with the three modes pinned as known answers: `nativeHz` reads the `relNs` sensor counter (**25.3400 Hz**) and the ms-stamp fallback is pinned as *measurably wrong* (**25.6410 Hz, +1.19 %**) so deleting that branch cannot pass quietly; a 20 % dropout moves the rate 0.00 Hz (count÷duration would read ~20 % low); the last peak keeps its **absolute time** through `toGrid` (297.00 s → 297.00 s, where a 5.07 Hz grid does not); one `flowChannel` pass attenuates 0.8 Hz by **−36.69 dB** vs −64.66 dB for two, passband −0.07 dB. Two of those reproduce figures this brief published independently (§2's 1.2 % error, §8's −36.6 dB), which is why they are known answers rather than guessed tolerances. Mutation-verified by reintroducing each original bug — **and one assertion was itself broken, caught only by mutating**: the §3 check first snapped the output peak to the nearest crest and *passed under the decimation mutation*, since decimation slides the peak onto a different crest. **Not built, with reasons recorded:** the shared `nativeHz` spine helper (inlined into every bundle → re-stamps all 8 `manifestHash` values for one caller) and the proposed "no DSP computes a rate as `n / durSec`" lint — scanned, and it has **no subjects**: the only count-over-duration expressions are genuine *event* rates (`oxydex-dsp.js:3079`, `oxydex-fusion.js:326`), inseparable from sample rates by source scan without the grandfather list `CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II` §4 removed | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-01)* |
| [`PPGDEX-OPTICAL-DETECTOR-AND-SIGMA-REDERIVE-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-OPTICAL-DETECTOR-AND-SIGMA-REDERIVE-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md) | §1 — the optical beat detector counted a prominent DIASTOLIC wave as a second beat (TERMA has no amplitude discrimination; at 48 bpm the intruder lands ~625 ms out, twice the fixed 0.30 s refractory), so optical HR read 2× true on 4 of 17 trio nights. Fixed by sizing the refractory from a windowed-autocorrelation cadence. §2 — re-derived the published Verity σ: the gate was MISDIAGNOSING harmonic doubling as "poor PPG contact" (cost 41% of the corpus, nights 10→15); σ confirmed 1.94→1.85, and the dead "6.2" prose traced + fixed. **DONE 2026-07-22 close-out:** the 4 doubled nights re-run on real raw Verity+H10 confirm OLD ratios 1.90–2.20 (`harmonic-double` band) → NEW ~1.00; `pickChannel` gained an inert-on-corpus `harmonicOutlierRefIdx` guard (gate-backed, 0 fixtures moved); the three residual §2 doc items closed as recorded conclusions (no other-paper edits — the SENSOR-TRIO CI curve is simulated, VERITY-SIGMA §7's non-resting need is orthogonal, SIGMA-REWRITE 1.94 confirmed) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`TRIO-ARTIFACT-GATE-AND-N15-POWER-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TRIO-ARTIFACT-GATE-AND-N15-POWER-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | The three-cornered hat is a VARIANCE estimator with no robustness to artifact: 3 bad epochs of 86 (3.5%) inflated σ_H10 from 2.5 → 9.6 bpm. Two σ estimates were wrong in OPPOSITE directions — Verity's CI optimistic (the quality gate was censoring the hard nights), H10's pessimistic (one 15-min artifact burst). Validated fix: a cross-corner consensus gate (AF-safe by construction) **⚠️ §3’s consensus-gate solution is DISPROVEN** — `TCH-FUSED-ROBUST-HAT` prototyped it on the real corpus (*"that gate … is either unreliable or biases the noisiest corner"*); build the fused-weight hat instead. §1/§2 findings and the N=15 power material STAND and still feed `SENSOR-TRIO-NIGHTS-PAPER`, which is why this is a scoped note and **not** a whole-brief `Superseded-by`. | Brief *(PROPOSED — §3 disproven 2026-07-18)* |
| [`TCH-FUSED-ROBUST-HAT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TCH-FUSED-ROBUST-HAT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | Executes `TRIO-ARTIFACT-GATE` with a better estimator. Prototyping on the real corpus showed the proposed hard consensus gate (and every single-cue fix) is unreliable or biases the noisiest corner. The validated answer: a **fused-weight hat** — per-second per-sensor confidence `c = density_trust × quality_trust` (DSP tier) drives a **weighted-variance** TCH. Ground truth: recovers 1.51/1.56/2.59 (planted 1.50/1.56/2.60), exact, unbiased, AF-safe, O(n), no corpus-tuned constant | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS)* |
| [`TCH-FUSED-ROBUST-HAT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TCH-FUSED-ROBUST-HAT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | Where the fused-hat findings generalize across the fleet: `RMSSD`/`SDNN`/`CV`/`MAGE` are all variance-family estimators with the SAME 0-breakdown fragility, and `beatConfidence` is signal-agnostic. Transfer map — ECGDex-own-HRV (drop-in), PulseDex/HRVDex robust HRV, OxyDex ODI / CPAPDex AHI artifact-event suppression, GlucoDex robust variability, revive the dead `bSQI` (detector B) | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`R5-HR-TRIPLET-REFERENCE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/R5-HR-TRIPLET-REFERENCE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | R5 executed: the HR triplet's independence test is NOT RUNNABLE — a hat cannot be validated by one of its OWN corners (σ_ECG²(TCH) ≡ cov of the other two corners' errors, verified to 6.7e-14, so the test has exactly zero power). What IS measurable is bias: OxyDex under-reads by −0.36 bpm, invisible to every published σ. The fix is one cable — connect the ResMed oximeter | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`PROFILED-HOTSPOTS-CI-AND-DSP-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PROFILED-HOTSPOTS-CI-AND-DSP-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | CPU-profiled ground truth, not op-counts. The CI `test` gate is ECGDex-bound and SINGLE-threaded — `lombScargle` alone is 35% of it. `DEX_GROUP` filters the REPORT, not the RUN, so CI sharding is not a workflow-only change. `PpgDex.compute()` spends 28% in `mean`, because `pearson()` recomputes `mean(tmpl)` once per beat. Plus §0: a synthetic microbenchmark lied by 18× — profile the real pipeline first | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21 — §2/§1a executed via CI-SHARDING; §3 retracted (vm-realm profile artifact, EFFICIENCY-AUDIT §M1); §4 dismissed; §1b decided: LEAVE `lombScargle` as-is)* |
| [`TRIO-POWER-N15-FINDINGS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TRIO-POWER-N15-FINDINGS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | Attempted SENSOR-TRIO-NIGHTS §181 (the N=10→15 power re-run). **The σ re-fit is NOT landed** — the planted σ and the paper's Tables 1–3 are ONE ATOMIC UNIT (those tables *are* the Monte-Carlo at that σ), so changing one without the other silently publishes a simulation of a truth nobody chose. **Blocker 1:** a 50k/100k re-run does not complete — the WebGPU lane accelerates only the N-sweep; the ρ/duration sweeps are CPU-bound (ETA advanced 1 min in 25 min at 100k). The paper is also self-inconsistent: Table 1 says 50,000 trials, the abstract says 720, the tool defaults to 500. **Blocker 2 (FIXED here):** the planted σ is TRIPLICATED (page/CPU/GPU) and was ungated — a stale cache made the tool REPORT σ=2.72/1.86/1.94 while SIMULATING at 2.60/1.58/1.85, and briefly faked a GPU≠CPU divergence (they are bit-identical). Measured-but-unlanded: the 15-night hat is **2.60/1.58/1.85** and moves 2 cells (H10 ±0.25: 1→2 — a *quieter* corner needs MORE windows, because the TCH couples them; Verity ±0.15: 3→2) | Brief *(PROPOSED)* |
| [`WORKER-REALM-GATES-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/WORKER-REALM-GATES-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | Follows PPGDEX-WORKER-CLOSURE. The `cadenceSamples` bug was not bad luck — **nothing in this repo executed a worker**. **The trap:** `detectChannelsAsync` falls back to serial on `w.onerror`, so a throwing worker still returns CORRECT NUMBERS — any check comparing worker output to serial **passes on a completely dead pool**. Demonstrated: against the pre-fix DSP the new rig reds on `NO worker threw` while `worker output ≡ serial` **still passes**. **§1** the PPG blob now EXECUTES in the headless gates (`new Function` reproduces the empty worker realm in both lanes) — necessary because the STATIC check said `CLOSED` on code that still threw `REFR_CADENCE_FRAC is not defined` (a module `const` does not travel with `Function.toString()`). **§2** a REAL-Worker rig in the browser lane (spawned · none errored · ≡ serial). **§3 ⚠️** `ecgdex-app.js`'s `WORKER_SRC` carries a **FOURTH, ungated `parseTimestamp`** (`_ckPF`) that runs ONLY above the 5 MB worker threshold — a drift there would corrupt timestamps **exclusively on large overnight recordings** while every fixture stayed green; measured to agree with `DexClock` on all 12 vendor formats, now pinned. Audit also: 10 surfaces driven in a real browser → **0 uncaught errors**; H2 (OverDex/Data-Unifier missing `PPGMorph`) **resolved as a non-issue**. Gates only — no re-bundle | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-12)* |
| [`PPGDEX-WORKER-CLOSURE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-WORKER-CLOSURE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | `Uncaught ReferenceError: cadenceSamples is not defined` (reported from the live demo). The PPG worker is a `blob:` built from an INLINED source string whose deps array is **hand-maintained**, and the worker realm starts **empty** — the optical-detector fix gave `detectBeats` an adaptive refractory sourced from a new `cadenceSamples()` + `REFR_CADENCE_FRAC`, and **neither was shipped**. **Why no gate saw it:** `w.onerror` falls back to serial, so the NUMBERS stayed right — and the equiv/headless gates run `analyze()`/`compute()`, which are serial BY DESIGN and **never touch the worker**. Symptoms: a console error, and all 3 LED channels detected on the MAIN THREAD (the exact thing the pool exists to prevent). A module `const` **does not travel with `Function.toString()`** — a static call-graph check missed `REFR_CADENCE_FRAC`; only RUNNING the worker realm found it. Verified worker ≡ serial on 3 real captures × 3 channels (9/9, byte-identical). New gate re-derives the closure from the DSP's own source text; reds against `origin/main` naming both symbols | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-12)* |
| [`PPGDEX-PI-AND-PARSE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-PI-AND-PARSE-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | Executes `EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-12` §C1+§P1. **A shipped app was showing a wrong number.** `ppgdex-morph.js` stepped its sample loop by `winSamp = winSec × fs`, and `fs` is the median sensor-ns delta — so on a real Polar file it is NEVER integral (176.26 → 10575.6). A fractional `Float32Array` index returns `undefined` → `NaN` → **`pi` silently `null`**: the surfaced per-window perfusion-index trend was plotting **1/2, 1/8, 2/7** windows on real captures (now 2/2, 8/8, 7/7). Rounding ONLY the sample bounds leaves beat→window assignment untouched (proved: `analyze()` hashes identically with `pi` stripped) and is **2.24×** faster. Plus §P1: `parsePPG` ran `parseTimestamp` on all ~190k rows for a `lastTs` only a degenerate fallback reads → **2.08×**, byte-identical. The audit's 3rd finding was **REJECTED** — it would have broken PAT feasibility (`pat-feasibility-worker.js` reads `tMs` on those rows). Pinned by a regression group using a NON-INTEGER `fs` (an integer `fs` passes even against the bug — which is why the suite never caught it) | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-12)* |
| [`PPGDEX-PI-AND-PARSE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-PI-AND-PARSE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) **§1 CLOSED 2026-08-01 — NOT ACTIONABLE: there is no consumer that can opt out.** The safe `opts.stamps` shape was implemented, measured and then REVERTED UNSHIPPED. Re-measured on a real 79.7 MB Verity companion (1.17 M rows): **1.78×**, not the proposed 2.08×, and below this section own ≥2× bar. The consumer census had gone stale — 2 named, **9 actual** (gaining resp-acc-analysis-app, motiondex-dsp, trio-batch, polar-sense-ppg) — which is precisely the hazard the section was written about. And **every one reads `tMs`**: decisively `tools/trio-batch.mjs:1299` sets `relNs: NaN` on every row it keeps, so `tMs` is its SOLE time base and opting it out drops every row. Nothing shipped because a `ppgdex-dsp.js` change costs PpgDex + both orchestrators re-bundled and fixture re-verification **for a knob no caller can pass** — and a dormant optimization is a standing invitation to opt a caller out later without redoing the audit, where the failure mode is silence | Residue from executing PPGDEX-PI-AND-PARSE. **§1** the companion-parse 2.08× (`parseSensorXYZ`), deferred not dismissed — needs a back-compat-safe `opts.stamps` shape because `pat-feasibility-worker.js:117` reads `r.tMs`. **§2** drop `ppgdex-morph.js` from biome's formatter-exempt list once it is genuinely reflowed. **§3** sweep the fleet for other float-stepped sample loops used as array indices — the failure mode is SILENT (`undefined`→`NaN`→`null`, no throw); candidate house-invariant lint. **§4** the synthetic fixtures all used an integer `fs`, so they could only prove the code works on inputs that never occur — audit the other nodes' generators for the same tidiness | Brief *(PROPOSED — deferred; §3/§4 EXECUTED; §1 blocked on gitignored ~600k-row companions; §2 conditional on a morph re-bundle)* |
| [`GATE-INTEGRITY-AND-DEVLOOP-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/GATE-INTEGRITY-AND-DEVLOOP-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | Executes `EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-12` §G1+§D1+§D2. **§G1** a ⊘ SKIP is neither pass nor fail, so the gate could shrink in SILENCE — the raw corpus is gitignored, so on a fresh clone **all 8 real-recording equivalence legs (the whole GATE-C surface) have never run on the merge path** (CI 2093/11-skipped, GATE B 10 of 23; local 2113/2, 23/23) and nothing pinned the skip count. Every skip must now be DECLARED in `tests/expected-skips.json` — an undeclared skip FAILS, fail-closed — plus a loud COVERAGE report and `DEX_UPLOADS=<path>` (which also makes CI's coverage reproducible locally). **§D1** `--jobs=N` forks the CI partition locally: **100.7 s → 24.9 s**, identical verdict (`npm run test:par`; `npm run check` uses it). **§D2** the two committed ledgers were a conflict ENGINE (45% of commits, 10 merge-resolutions in 48 h) because scalar `count`/`generated` changed on BOTH sides of any disjoint add — and a conflicted PR gets **NO CI AT ALL** (GitHub builds `pull_request` against the merge commit; it hit PR #43 AND #46 in one session). Now line-oriented text + `merge=union`, single-sourced in `tests/list-format.js`; proved in a scratch repo to auto-merge where the JSON conflicted, with the `list == fs` staleness assertion as the backstop | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-12)* |
| [`TRIO-BATCH-O2RING-DAT-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TRIO-BATCH-O2RING-DAT-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md) | **Six analyzable nights were invisible because the oximeter's CSV export stopped.** `trio-batch` discovered the oxy corner with `.csv` only — and *anchors the whole night on it*, so a night with a complete ECG+PPG pair was dropped for want of an anchor. The O2Ring writes a **native `.dat` beside the vendor CSV**, and when the export stops (app not opened, phone not synced) the `.dat` is all that survives: **20 CSV-less `.dat`s** sat in the capture folder, 6 of them (07-07 … 07-12) with full concurrent Polar streams. **OxyDex has read this format all along** (`isO2RingBin` / `decodeO2RingBinToCSV`, live on the browser drop path) — only the *headless* corner was blind, because `compute()` takes `{samples\|rows\|text}` and never bytes. The fix **exposes the existing decoder rather than copying it** (a second implementation of the 3-byte layout / `0xFF 0xFF` trailer / `motion×2` scale would drift), prefers the vendor CSV when both files exist, and ranks the anchor by **duration, not bytes** — bytes stopped being comparable the moment a `.dat` (~10× denser) could compete with a CSV. Equivalence is *proved*, not asserted: 2026-07-06 has both files, and same-code CSV-path ≡ `.dat`-path with **zero diffs** across every metric, event and timeseries value. Failure was **silent by construction** — one `⊘` among hundreds of `·` lines, exit 0; the silent-drop ergonomics are deliberately left to their own brief | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-13)* |
| [`CI-SHARDING-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CI-SHARDING-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | Executes PROFILED-HOTSPOTS §2→§1a. The 4-minute `test` gate was ONE single-threaded step at 104% CPU on a multi-core runner; now 4 parallel shards — **4m05s → ~1m10s** (101.3 s → 25.3 s makespan, 4.00x). `group()` now skips *execution*, not just the report (so `--group` finally means what it claimed). The shards **partition** the suite — index-based, never name-based, because a pattern shard silently drops any group no pattern claims and every shard still goes green. `verify-shard-union.mjs` proves the partition on every push (0.08 s) and `--deep` proves the shard-union is assertion-for-assertion identical to the unsharded run. `genSynthetic` memoisation deliberately NOT taken: those groups regenerate on purpose to assert byte-reproducibility, so a cache would speed the gate up by weakening it | Brief *(DONE — 2026-07-12)* |
| [`TEST-COVERAGE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TEST-COVERAGE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | Two-pass coverage audit — modules that **ship but are never asserted**, outside the well-covered DSP/clock/contract/provenance backbone. **Item 1 (landing here):** the five per-node **profile engines** (~2,850 lines of *independent* cited physiology — Tanaka HRmax, Uth–Sørensen VO₂, age-norm RMSSD/RHR, CVHR apnea bands, GMI↔A1c/eAG bias) had **zero behavioral assertions**; `ECGProfile`/`GLUProfile`/`PPGProfile` are now surfaced into both runners' `env` and pinned by a `personalize()` known-answer group (test-only, no re-bundle). **Item 2:** `nsrr-adapter.js` (204) PSG ingest parser — untested. **Item 3:** 4 analysis kernels (`qrs-yield`/`qrs-equiv`/`pat-feasibility`/`cohort-regression`) appear only in `.html` static lists, their math never executed. **Item 4:** worker≡serial proven for only 2 of 6 (`cohort`/`pat-feasibility`/`qrs-*` workers unguarded). **Item 5 (minor):** `overdex-walk` untested; `support.js` is an orphan (source `dc-runtime/` absent, inlined nowhere) → cleanup not test. Item 1b (HRVDex/OxyDex profile seam, re-bundle) + 2–5 land independently | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-27 — items 1b/3/4 all executed in `-II` 2026-07-21; two of this brief's own premises were WRONG: 1b needed no seam/re-bundle (both profile modules already leak pure kernels as headless globals) and 3/4 needed no `analysis-stats` extraction (kernels reachable as-is; workers gated as real-Worker known-answer rigs))* |
| [`TEST-COVERAGE-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TEST-COVERAGE-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | Continues the parent's **items 3/4/1b**, which execution showed are **NOT test-only** (each needs a source change). **Item 3** (analysis kernels `qrs-yield`/`qrs-equiv`/`pat-feasibility`/`cohort-regression`): DOM page-controllers with no export seam that duplicate their own `pearson`/`ba`/`olsR2` — a tested copy in `analysis-stats.js` would be *fake coverage*; plan = **Route A** expose-the-shipped-function + DOM-guard + known-answer (lower risk), or **Route B** extract-to-shared-kernel + delegate (also dedups, touches page HTML/CSP gates). **Item 4** (worker≡serial): the 4 workers are separate FILES with **no serial twin** to diff — plan = a known-answer **real-Worker rig** (`WORKER-REALM-GATES §2`), not a hollow ≡-serial gate. **Item 1b** (HRVDex/OxyDex profile seam): DOM-only, no namespace — add an additive pure surface → **re-bundle + provenance regen** (the one item that pays that cost). Direct application of `TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md` ("test the shipped code, never a copy") | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21 — items 3/4/1b executed+gated (olsR2/qrs-equiv KA · HRVDex+OxyDex profile seams, NO re-bundle · qrs-equiv+qrs-yield worker rigs); cohort/pat-feasibility workers consciously deferred)* |

## Maintaining this index

When you add a root-level `*.md`, add one row here in the matching group with its real H1 and a
role tag. When a brief is fully executed and superseded, move it to `docs-archive/` and drop its row
(or relabel it *Historical*). This file is the one place a newcomer should land before opening any
single brief.

Every relative link in this file is gate-checked (`docs-ledger` check 4 — CLAUDE.md §📌): a `briefs/` link resolves against the real brief set (4a), and every other `docs/·audits/·wiring/·root` link resolves
against a whole-tree path inventory (4b) the Node lane recomputes from disk on each run. If you move or
rename a linked file, just fix the link — there is no committed inventory to regenerate (the gate reads the
tree straight from the filesystem; CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II §4).
